Method: 使用具有严格因果记忆核的生成模型，分析时间不对称随机过程在相似空间中产生的尖锐转变，推导出不对称过渡轮廓的解析表达式A(θ)=A₀cos(θ+δ)。

Result: 模型预测与高精度计数实验数据高度吻合（χ²/dof=0.50，p=0.86），实验显示出极显著的相关性（p<10⁻¹⁵），验证了相似空间方法检测隐藏因果记忆的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究确立了谱分析对非厄米、非平稳过程的基本局限性，为开放量子系统中的因果记忆提供了实验可检验的特征，开辟了检测隐藏时间相关性的新途径。

Abstract: We identify a new class of non-Hermitian causal processes that produce statistically significant temporal correlations invisible to conventional spectral methods. Using a generative model with a strictly causal memory kernel, we demonstrate that time-asymmetric stochastic processes naturally yield sharp transitions at characteristic scales that appear as localized structures in similarity space but leave no trace in power spectra. The model predicts an asymmetric transition profile with orientation-dependent asymmetry parameter $A(θ)=A_0\cos(θ+δ)$ and achieves quantitative agreement ($χ^2/\mathrm{dof}=0.50$, $p=0.86$) with high-precision counting experiments exhibiting $p<10^{-15}$ significance. These results establish a fundamental limitation of spectral analysis for non-Hermitian, non-stationary processes and provide experimentally testable signatures of causal memory in open quantum systems.

</details>


### [77] [Longest weakly increasing subsequences of discrete random walks on the integers with heavy tailed distribution of increments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29047)
*José Ricardo G. Mendonça,Marcelo V. Freire*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究重尾随机游走的最长弱递增子序列长度标度行为，发现方差有限时标度为√n log n，方差无限时标度为n^θ(θ>0.5)，分布近似对数正态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有重尾增量分布的随机游走的最长弱递增子序列长度行为，特别是当增量分布具有无限方差时，扩展先前对重尾随机游走LIS的研究

Method: 使用探索性拟合、加权非线性最小二乘和ANOVA模型比较，分析不同α参数下的重尾随机游走，并与简单随机游走(α>log₂n)对比

Result: 当增量分布方差有限(α>2)时，平均长度标度为⟨L_n⟩∼√n log n；方差无限(α≤2)时，标度为⟨L_n⟩∼n^θ(θ>0.5)。L_n分布主体近似对数正态，但尾部存在系统偏差

Conclusion: 结果证实并扩展了先前对重尾随机游走LIS的研究，提出了L_n分布是否由对数正态分布给出或有效描述的问题

Abstract: We investigate the behavior of the length of the longest weakly increasing subsequences (weak LIS) of $n$-step random walks with nonzero integer increments $k = \pm 1, \pm 2, \dots$ given by a zero-mean, symmetric heavy tailed mass distribution proportional to $|k|^{-1-α}$ for several values of the real parameter $α> 0$ together with that of the simple random walk ($k=\pm 1$), to which the $n$-step heavy tailed random walks tends when $α> (1+o(1))\log_{2}{n}$.
  By means of exploratory fits, weighted nonlinear least squares, and ANOVA model comparison, we found that the sample average length $\langle{L_{n}}\rangle$ scales like $\langle{L_{n}}\rangle \sim \sqrt{n}\log{n}$ when the distribution of increments has finite variance ($α> 2$) and $\langle{L_{n}}\rangle \sim n^θ$ with a varying exponent $θ> 0.5$ when the variance is infinite ($α\leq 2$). Distributional diagnostics indicate that the bulk of the $L_{n}$ distribution is very well-approximated by a lognormal model, though systematic deviations are observed in the tails.
  Our results corroborate and expand upon previous results for the LIS of other types of heavy-tailed random walks and raise a conjecture as to whether the distribution of $L_{n}$ is given, or can be effectively described, by a lognormal distribution.

</details>


### [78] [How much of persistent homology is topology? A quantitative decomposition for spin model phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29072)
*Matthew Loftus*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究量化了点云持续同调(PH)信号中拓扑成分的比例，发现在相变检测中H_0统计量主要受密度驱动，而H_1统计量包含显著拓扑信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云持续同调已广泛应用于经典自旋模型的相变检测，但尚未量化PH信号中真正拓扑成分的比例。研究旨在区分密度驱动和拓扑驱动的贡献，评估现有方法检测相变的机制。

Method: 引入f_topo定量分解方法，通过比较真实自旋构型与密度匹配的随机零模型，分离PH统计量中的密度驱动和拓扑贡献。应用于2D Ising模型(L=16-128，十个温度)和Potts模型(q=3,5)，分析H_0和H_1统计量。

Result: H_0统计量(总持续性、持续性熵、特征计数)94-100%为密度驱动(f_topo<0.07)；密度匹配随机零模型能检测到与真实构型相同的临界温度Tc和可比峰值高度。H_1统计量部分为拓扑驱动：拓扑分数随系统尺寸增长δ(TP_H1)~L^0.53，遵循有限尺寸标度塌缩；最长持续性条带具有强拓扑性(f_topo>1)并与关联长度标度相关。

Conclusion: 相变检测中H_0统计量主要反映密度信息而非拓扑结构，建议TDA社区采用随机零模型作为标准实践；当需要真正拓扑信息时应使用H_1而非H_0统计量。拓扑超额随系统尺寸增大从大尺度特征向小尺度特征转移。

Abstract: Point-cloud persistent homology (PH) -- computing alpha or Rips complexes on spin-position point clouds -- has been widely applied to detect phase transitions in classical spin models since Donato et al. (2016), with subsequent studies attributing the detection to the topological content of the persistence diagram. We ask a simple question that has not been posed: what fraction of the PH signal is genuinely topological? We introduce f_topo, a quantitative decomposition that separates the density-driven and topological contributions to any PH statistic by comparing real spin configurations against density-matched shuffled null models. Across the 2D Ising model (system sizes L = 16-128, ten temperatures) and Potts models (q = 3, 5), we find that H_0 statistics -- total persistence, persistence entropy, feature count -- are 94-100% density-driven (f_topo < 0.07). The density-matched shuffled null detects T_c at the identical location and with comparable peak height as real configurations, showing that density alone is sufficient for phase transition detection. However, H_1 statistics are partially topological: the topological fraction grows with system size as delta(TP_{H_1}) ~ L^{0.53} and follows a finite-size scaling collapse delta(T, L) = L^{0.53} g(tL^{1/nu}) with collapse quality CV = 0.27. The longest persistence bar is strongly topological (f_topo > 1) and scales with the correlation length. A scale-resolved analysis reveals that the topological excess shifts from large-scale to small-scale features as L increases. We propose that the TDA-for-phase-transitions community adopt shuffled null models as standard practice, and that H_1 rather than H_0 statistics be used when genuine topological information is sought.

</details>


### [79] [The geometric origin of criticality: a universal mechanism in mean-field rotor Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29074)
*Loris Di Cairano*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于能量壳几何结构的平均场转子哈密顿量临界性通用判据，将相变重新表述为能量壳的几何不稳定性现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上通过热力学奇点来表征相变，但作者认为相关信息已编码在能量壳沿特定集体方向的几何重组方式中。他们希望建立一个几何上通用的框架，独立于模型具体细节，仅依赖于有限的集体耦合参数。

Method: 针对有限维三角平均场相互作用的一类广泛系统，作者推导了Weingarten算子迹（代表主曲率）在序参量振幅方面的通用集体展开。该展开定义了一个能量依赖的二次型，其特征模识别系统的几何不稳定通道。临界性与相应曲率系数的消失相关联，从而为在相变中变得不稳定的模式提供了直接的几何选择原理。

Result: 该方法恢复了标准平均场转子模型中已知的临界通道，并自然地扩展到多模和谱耦合情况。结果表明，临界行为可以理解为由底层能量壳几何的集体重组触发的能量壳几何重组。

Conclusion: 平均场系统中的相变（通常为一阶或二阶）被重新表述为微正则能量壳固有的几何不稳定性现象。所得框架在所考虑的类别内具有几何普适性，除有限集体耦合集外独立于模型具体细节，为理解临界行为提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: We introduce a universal criterion for criticality in mean-field rotor Hamiltonians based on the geometric structure of the constant-energy shell. Rather than characterizing the onset of a phase transition through the conventional thermodynamic singularities alone, we show that the relevant information is already encoded in the way the geometry of the shell reorganizes along distinguished collective directions.
  For a broad class of finite-dimensional trigonometric mean-field interactions, the trace of the Weingarten operator (representing the principal curvatures) admits a universal collective expansion in terms of the order-parameter amplitudes. This expansion defines an energy-dependent quadratic form whose eigenmodes identify the geometrically unstable channels of the system. Criticality is then associated with the vanishing of the corresponding curvature coefficients, yielding a direct geometric selection principle for the modes that become unstable at the transition.
  In this way, the phase transition in mean-field systems (usually of first- or second-order) is reformulated as a geometric instability phenomenon intrinsic to the microcanonical energy shell. The resulting framework is geometrically universal within the class considered, independent of model-specific details except for a finite set of collective couplings. Moreover, our approach recovers the known critical channels in standard mean-field rotor models while extending naturally to multimode and spectrally coupled cases. These results support a view in which critical behavior can be understood as reorganizations of energy-shell geometry triggered by a collective restructuring of the underlying energy-shell geometry.

</details>


### [80] [Mean first passage times of velocity jump processes in higher dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29241)
*Maria R. D'Orsogna,Alan E. Lindsay,Thomas Hillen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用框架，用于估计固定速度速度跳跃过程的平均首次通过时间和生存概率的高阶矩，解决了定向偏置下高维速度跳跃过程首次通过性质的理论空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次通过现象在物理、生物和金融领域广泛存在，但现有研究主要关注扩散运动。许多随机过程更适合用速度跳跃过程描述（具有持续性运动被随机速度变化打断的特性），然而在定向偏置下的高维速度跳跃过程的首次通过性质研究不足，需要建立通用理论框架。

Method: 提出了一个通用框架来估计固定速度速度跳跃过程的平均首次通过时间和生存概率的高阶矩。方法涵盖从强对齐到完全角度各向异性的可能重定向范围。对于低克努森数情况（平均自由程远小于到目标的距离），推导了平均首次通过时间的通用形式，其中两个偏置函数编码了广泛的角分布类别。在窄捕获极限下，分析了定向持续性引起的异常标度行为。最后获得了能够准确再现首次通过统计量的朗之万表示。

Result: 推导出了平均首次通过时间的通用形式，其中两个偏置函数能够编码包括von Mises-Fisher、包裹柯西和椭圆族在内的广泛角分布类别。在窄捕获极限下，发现定向持续性会引起异常标度行为，包括平均首次通过时间保持有限而标准扩散理论预测发散的特殊区域。获得了能够准确再现首次通过统计量的朗之万表示。所有分析预测都通过数值模拟得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为定向偏置下高维速度跳跃过程的首次通过性质提供了通用理论框架，填补了该领域的理论空白。研究揭示了定向持续性在窄捕获极限下会引起异常标度行为，并建立了能够准确描述首次通过统计量的朗之万表示，为理解广泛物理、生物和金融系统中的首次通过现象提供了新工具。

Abstract: First passage phenomena arise across physics, biology, and finance when stochastic processes first reach a threshold, triggering downstream events. Examples include the irreversible exit from a domain, a biochemical reaction, a financial selloff. While typical formulations involve diffusive motion, many stochastic processes are better described as velocity jump processes, characterized by persistent motion interrupted by stochastic velocity changes. Despite their ubiquity, first-passage properties of velocity jump processes remain underdeveloped in higher dimensions, especially under directional bias. We present a general framework to estimate the mean first passage time (MFPT) and higher moments of the survival probability for fixed-speed velocity jump processes where possible reorientations range from strong alignment to full angular anisotropy. For low Knudsen numbers, when the mean free path is small compared to the distance to the target, we derive a universal form for the MFPT in which two bias functions encode broad classes of angular distributions, including von Mises-Fisher, wrapped Cauchy, and elliptical families. In the narrow capture limit of a vanishingly small target, directional persistence induces anomalous scaling, including regimes where the MFPT remains finite whereas standard diffusion would predict divergence. Finally, we obtain a Langevin representation that accurately reproduces first-passage statistics. Analytical predictions are confirmed by numerical simulations.

</details>


### [81] [Force Geometry and Irreversibility in Nonequilibrium Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29416)
*Erez Aghion,Swetamber Das*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出力几何作为非平衡热力学的组织原则，通过力对齐作为不可逆性的几何决定因素，解释了实验中观察到的波动-耗散反相关和非均匀耗散现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标量随机热力学框架虽然能通过电流和熵产率量化耗散，但无法揭示塑造空间耗散模式的底层矢量力几何结构。实验观察到的异质耗散与局部位置波动的反相关性暴露了这一结构空白。

Method: 引入力对齐作为不可逆性的几何决定因素，形式化通过时间相关的力相关系数量化确定性驱动力与熵梯度之间的相对取向。构建几何控制图，在力空间表示中定位实验操作点。

Result: 熵产不仅取决于力的大小，还取决于确定性驱动力与熵梯度之间的相对取向，仅在精确反对齐且大小匹配时才消失。建立了熵产的瞬时几何下界，即使在力大小匹配时也有效。在过阻尼动力学中，完美反对齐定义了热力学停滞点。

Conclusion: 力几何为不可逆性提供了统一的结构视角，解释了实验观察到的波动-耗散反相关和非均匀耗散，可应用于活性生物系统、微流变学，并自然扩展到欠阻尼动力学。

Abstract: Recent experiments have revealed heterogeneous dissipation in optically trapped systems, often anticorrelated with local positional fluctuations, exposing a structural gap in the scalar stochastic thermodynamic description. While the conventional scalar framework successfully quantifies dissipation through currents and entropy production rates, it does not reveal the underlying vectorial force geometry that shapes spatial dissipation patterns. Here, we bridge this gap by identifying force geometry as an organizing principle for nonequilibrium thermodynamics and introducing force alignment as a geometric determinant of irreversibility. We show that entropy production depends not only on force magnitudes but also on the relative orientation between deterministic driving forces and entropic gradients, vanishing only under exact anti-alignment with matched magnitudes. We formalize this geometric alignment through a time-dependent force-correlation coefficient, quantifying the relative orientation between the forces. This yields an instantaneous geometric lower bound on entropy production that remains valid even when force magnitudes are matched. For overdamped dynamics, perfect anti-alignment defines a thermodynamic stall where net transport vanishes and the lower bound on entropy production is saturated. This force-level perspective provides a structural explanation for the experimentally observed fluctuation-dissipation anticorrelation and nonuniform dissipation. We construct geometric control charts for both constant dragging and sinusoidal driving protocols, explicitly locating experimental operating points within this force-space representation. Together, these results position force geometry as a unifying structural perspective on irreversibility, spanning active biological systems, microrheology, and naturally extending to underdamped dynamics.

</details>


### [82] [Fundamental problems in Statistical Physics XIV: Lecture on Correlation and response functions in statistical physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29481)
*Thomas Franosch*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 介绍物理学家视角下的(自)相关函数和线性响应函数，讨论经典物理中的涨落耗散定理，并探讨相关函数的一般特征化方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理文献中对相关函数的一般特征化方法缺乏广泛认识，尽管数学文献中已有优雅且长期确立的结果（Bochner定理用于相关函数，Herglotz-Nevanlinna表示用于响应函数）。作者旨在弥合这一知识差距，将数学工具引入物理教育中。

Method: 分为两部分：第一部分以物理学家语言介绍(自)相关函数和线性响应函数，重点讨论经典涨落耗散定理；第二部分采用更严格的视角，阐述相关函数的关键结构性质并提供选定推导，最后讨论响应函数的一般特征化结果。

Result: 通过讲座笔记形式，系统介绍了相关函数和响应函数的数学特征化理论，特别是Bochner定理对相关函数的特征化和Herglotz-Nevanlinna表示对响应函数的特征化，为物理学家提供了理解这些函数一般性质的数学工具。

Conclusion: 相关函数和响应函数的一般特征化理论在数学文献中已完善建立，但在物理界未得到足够重视。这些讲座笔记旨在将Bochner定理和Herglotz-Nevanlinna表示等数学工具引入物理教育，帮助物理学家超越具体动力学定律来理解相关函数的基本性质。

Abstract: In the first part of these short lecture notes, we will present an introduction on (auto-)correlation functions and linear-response functions in the language of a physicist. In particular, the fluctuation-dissipation theorem in classical physics is presented underlining the central role of correlation functions. The fundamental importance of (auto-)correlation functions raises the natural question on how they are characterized in general without referring to the concrete underlying dynamical laws. Perhaps unexpectedly -- despite being elegant and long established in the mathematical literature (Bochner's theorem for correlations; Herglotz-Nevanlinna representations for response) -- this answer is not widely appreciated in physics, partly because the requisite tools lie outside the standard curriculum.
  In the second part we adopt a more rigorous viewpoint: we state the key structural properties of correlation functions and provide selected derivations of these results. Finally, we return to linear response and discuss general characterization results for response functions.

</details>


### [83] [Thermalization in high-dimensional systems: the (weak) role of chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29614)
*Marco Baldovin,Marco Cattaneo,Dario Lucente,Paolo Muratore-Ginanneschi,Angelo Vulpiani*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视统计力学有效性的关键因素，认为大自由度数和广延可观测量比动力学细节更重要，并将这一观点扩展到非平衡情况，研究谐振子和非谐振子链的热化特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受FPUT问题启发，论文旨在探讨统计力学预测在缺乏严格遍历性情况下仍然成立的原因。作者认为统计力学的有效性主要依赖于大自由度数和广延可观测量，而非动力学细节，并希望将这一观点从平衡态扩展到非平衡态。

Method: 研究谐振子（可积）和非谐振子（混沌）链在初始非平衡条件下的热化特性。通过分析不同可观测量在两种系统中的行为，验证Khinchin关于宏观可观测量典型性证明的扩展。

Result: 在可积系统中，热化是否发生取决于可观测量；在混沌系统中，所有可观测量最终都会达到热化，但弛豫时间尺度可能超过观测时间。

Conclusion: 统计力学的有效性主要依赖于大自由度数和广延可观测量，而非严格的遍历性。这一结论在平衡态和非平衡态都成立，为理解统计力学预测的普适性提供了新视角。

Abstract: In their seminal work, Fermi, Pasta, Ulam and Tsingou explored the connection between statistical mechanics and dynamical properties, such as chaos and ergodicity. Even today, seventy years later, the topic is not fully understood: while most results of statistical mechanics require the ergodic hypothesis to be rigorously proved, there are many indications that these predictions, both in and out of equilibrium, hold even in the absence of a rigorous form of ergodicity.
  Motivated by the above considerations, in this work we reconsider the point of view that the relevant ingredients for the validity of statistical mechanics are the large number of degrees of freedom and the choice of extensive observables, while the details of the dynamics do not play an essential role. This is the idea behind Khinchin's famous proof of the typicality of macroscopic observables at equilibrium. We extend this perspective to the context of non equilibrium, by investigating the thermalization properties of both harmonic (integrable) and nonharmonic (chaotic) oscillator chains initially prepared in out-of-equilibrium conditions. In integrable systems, thermalization occurs, or not, depending on the observable. In the chaotic regime, instead, thermalization is reached by any observable, although the relaxation timescale might be larger than the observation time.

</details>


### [84] [Optimal Control of a Mesoscopic Information Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29804)
*Emanuele Panizon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文解析求解了在代价高昂测量下通过光镊驱动过阻尼粒子的有限时间控制问题，建立了介观信息引擎的POMDP框架，推导出最优反馈控制律，并揭示了测量截止导致的"盲视"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究介观尺度下信息引擎的有限时间控制问题，特别是在测量成本高昂的情况下，如何通过光学陷阱驱动过阻尼粒子，探索从热涨落中提取最大功率的理论极限。

Method: 将介观信息引擎建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)，利用线性二次高斯(LQG)动力学将最优测量和驱动协议简化为代数Riccati递推关系，推导出陷阱位置的最优反馈控制律。

Result: 推导出最优反馈控制律，恢复了连续时间开环极限下的Schmiedl-Seifert驱动协议；绘制了引擎操作相空间，给出了从热涨落中提取最大功率的物理界限；发现了截止时间导致的"盲视"现象；将结果推广到可变精度传感器。

Conclusion: 该研究为介观信息引擎的有限时间控制提供了完整的解析解，揭示了测量成本、截止时间和传感器精度对引擎性能的影响，为实际信息引擎的设计和优化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We analytically solve the finite-time control problem of driving an overdamped particle via an optical trap under costly measurement. By formulating this mesoscopic information engine within the Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) framework, we demonstrate that the underlying Linear-Quadratic-Gaussian (LQG) dynamics reduce the optimal measurement and driving protocols to a one-dimensional algebraic Riccati recurrence. From this reduction, we derive the optimal feedback control law for the trap placement, which recovers the discontinuous Schmiedl-Seifert driving protocol in the continuous-time, open-loop limit. We map the operational phase space of the engine, deriving explicit physical bounds on the maximum power that can be extracted from thermal fluctuations. Taking the infinite-horizon limit, we find the exact periodic measurement schedules for the steady-state and derive the macroscopic velocity envelopes beyond which viscous drag forces the engine into a net-dissipative regime. We prove the emergence of deadline-induced blindness, a phenomenon where all measurement ceases as the deadline approaches regardless of their cost. Finally, we generalize the results to a variable-precision sensor.

</details>


### [85] [Bethe Ansatz with a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29932)
*Balázs Pozsgay,István Vona*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: LLM成功求解了三个未发表的可积自旋链模型的坐标Bethe Ansatz解，其中两个是新模型，展示了LLM在数学物理计算任务中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型在数学物理计算任务中的能力，特别是求解可积自旋链模型的坐标Bethe Ansatz解，这些模型的解之前未发表。

Method: 使用ChatGPT 5.2 Pro和5.4 Pro，让LLM半自主地求解三个可积哈密顿量的坐标Bethe Ansatz解，研究人员纠正过程中的错误，并通过精确对角化和人工验证结果。

Result: LLM成功求解了所有三个模型：第一个模型是标准可积模型；第二个模型明显破坏左右对称性但具有PT对称性；第三个模型通过特殊形式的嵌套Bethe Ansatz求解，具有无U(1)对称性的自由费米子结构，这种结构是独特的且由LLM发现。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效辅助求解数学物理中的复杂计算问题，特别是在发现新结构和求解未发表模型方面展现出潜力，但需要人类监督纠正错误。

Abstract: We explore the capability of a Large Language Model (LLM) to perform specific computations in mathematical physics: the task is to compute the coordinate Bethe Ansatz solution of selected integrable spin chain models. We select three integrable Hamiltonians for which the solutions were unpublished; two of the Hamiltonians are actually new. We observed that the LLM semi-autonomously solved the task in all cases, with a few mistakes along the way. These were corrected after the human researchers spotted them. The results of the LLM were checked against exact diagonalization (performed by separate programs), and the derivations were also checked by the authors. The Bethe Ansatz solutions are interesting in themselves. Our second model manifestly breaks left-right invariance, but it is PT-symmetric, therefore its solution could be interesting for applications in Generalized Hydrodynamics. And our third model is solved by a special form of the nested Bethe Ansatz, where the model is interacting, but the nesting level has a free fermionic structure lacking $U(1)$-invariance. This structure appears to be unique and it was found by the LLM. We used ChatGPT 5.2 Pro and 5.4 Pro by OpenAI.

</details>

### [86] [HAMMR-L: Noise Reduction in Quantum Outcomes Using a Richardson-Lucy Deconvolution Algorithm for Quantum State Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28821)
*Jake Scally,Austin Myers,Ryan Carmichael,Phat Tran,Xiuwen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: HAMMR-L：一种基于Richardson-Lucy图像去卷积的量子计算后处理技术，通过在汉明距离连接的状态图上应用图像处理方法来提高NISQ时代量子计算机输出分布的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算机存在显著噪声，特别是随着电路深度和量子比特数量增加而加剧。先前研究表明错误结果在汉明空间中表现出特定行为，这为改进NISQ时代计算机的输出分布提供了可能性。

Method: 提出HAMMR-L后处理技术，在测量结果的状态图上应用Richardson-Lucy图像去卷积方法，该状态图通过汉明距离连接。该方法与电路和硬件无关，不依赖特定硬件特性。

Result: HAMMR-L的初步实现优于现有的尖端汉明基后处理器（如QBEEP），且具有电路和硬件无关的优势（而QBEEP不具备此特性）。该方法显示出未来改进的明确潜力。

Conclusion: HAMMR-L作为一种原则性的后处理技术，通过将图像处理技术应用于量子测量结果的汉明空间表示，有效提高了输出分布的保真度。该方法具有通用性，并为进一步改进提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Current quantum computers present significant noise, especially as circuit depth and qubit count increase. Prior work has demonstrated that erroneous outcomes exhibit some behavior in Hamming space, enabling improvements in the output distributions of NISQ-era computers. We present HAMMR-L: a principled post-processing technique for improving the fidelity of output distributions by applying Richardson-Lucy image deconvolution on a state graph of measurement results connected by Hamming distance. We show that this preliminary implementation of HAMMR-L outperforms existing cutting-edge Hamming-based post-processors such as QBEEP while being circuit and hardware agnostic, which QBEEP is not. HAMMR-L also demonstrates clear potential for future improvements and we discuss how such improvements might be realized while highlighting the strengths, limitations, and generality of the underlying concept.

</details>


### [87] [Quantum Coherence and Giant Enhancement of Positron Channeling Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28827)
*Michael Shatnev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子力学计算正电子在平面谐波势中的沟道辐射，考虑不同横向能级间跃迁振幅的干涉效应，发现相干增强因子可达12-31倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Zhevago-Kumakhov模型将沟道辐射视为不同横向能级跃迁的独立贡献，忽略了量子相干效应。本文旨在研究正电子在近谐波沟道势中产生的相干辐射增强机制。

Method: 采用量子力学方法计算正电子在金刚石(110)平面沟道中的辐射，沟道势近似为抛物线势，横向能谱等间距。考虑正电子突然进入晶体形成的Glauber相干态，计算不同能级跃迁振幅的相干叠加。

Result: 相干辐射强度I_coh ∝ |A_j|²，比非相干(Zhevago-Kumakhov)结果增强因子G=12-31倍（正电子能量4-14 GeV）。数值结果与Avakyan等人实验峰值位置一致。辐射强度从N标度转变为N²标度。

Conclusion: 正电子在近谐波沟道势中产生显著的相干辐射增强，这一效应是正电子特有的。提出的非线性角度依赖性实验可验证相干模型。量子相干驱动的辐射增强为核物理和材料科学开辟了高强度单色伽马射线源的新途径。

Abstract: We present a quantum-mechanical calculation of positron channeling radiation in a planar harmonic potential, explicitly accounting for the interference between transition amplitudes from different transverse energy levels. Because the planar channel potential for positrons in diamond~(110) is well approximated by a parabola, the transverse spectrum is equidistant, $\varepsilon_n = Ω(n+\tfrac{1}{2})$, and all $n \to n{-}j$ transitions radiate at the same Doppler-shifted frequency. The entry of the positron into the crystal under the sudden approximation creates a Glauber coherent state with population amplitudes $c_n$. Phase synchronization between the $c_n$ and the dipole matrix elements ensures that all occupied levels contribute constructively to the radiation amplitude, giving an intensity $I_{\rm coh} \propto |A_j|^2$ that exceeds the incoherent (Zhevago--Kumakhov) result by a factor $\mathcal{G} = 12\text{--}31$ for positron energies of $4\text{--}14$~GeV in diamond~(110). Numerical results agree with the experimental peak positions of Avakyan \emph{et al.}~\cite{Avakyan1982}. The enhancement is unique to positrons because their nearly harmonic channel potential is not replicated for electrons. We propose a decisive experimental test of the coherent model based on the predicted nonlinear angular dependence of the peak intensity. The transition from $N$- to $N^2$-scaling of the radiated intensity, driven by quantum coherence, opens a route toward high-intensity monochromatic gamma-ray sources for nuclear physics and materials science.

</details>


### [88] [Geometric structure of the relativistic quantum phase space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28836)
*Philippe Manjakasoa Randriantsoa,Ravo Tokiniaina Ranaivoson,Raoelina Andriambololona,Roland Raboanary,Wilfrid Chrysante Solofoarisina,Anjary Feno Hasina Rasamimanana*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了(1,4)签名下的相对论量子相空间几何结构，构建了由平均值和逆协方差矩阵构成的标量不变量，并证明了其在线性正则变换下的不变性。该不变量编码了两个基本长度尺度，分别对应最大和最小坐标不确定性。通过分析渐近极限，揭示了与弯曲时空几何和弯曲动量空间结构的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论量子相空间形式体系将经典相空间扩展到包含量子态的平均值和方差-协方差矩阵，为不确定性原理和相对论协变性提供了统一框架。本研究旨在探索(1,4)签名下量子相空间的基本几何结构，建立量子相空间几何与宇宙学、量子引力之间的联系。

Method: 1. 在(1,4)签名下构建量子相空间的几何结构；2. 构造由平均值和逆方差-协方差矩阵构成的标量不变量；3. 证明该不变量在线性正则变换下的不变性；4. 对于满足不确定性关系的量子态，分析不变量编码的两个基本长度尺度；5. 推导统一平均值和量子涨落的几何方程；6. 分析两个渐近极限，分别对应弯曲时空几何和弯曲动量空间结构。

Result: 1. 成功构建了在线性正则变换下不变的标量不变量；2. 对于满足不确定性关系的量子态，该不变量编码了两个基本长度尺度：表征最大坐标不确定性的大尺度和表征最小坐标不确定性的小尺度；3. 从不变性推导出统一平均值和量子涨落的几何方程；4. 两个渐近极限分别导致：a) 与当前宇宙学观测一致的弯曲时空几何；b) 弯曲动量空间结构；5. 结果与玻恩互易原理一致，并与量子相空间和中微子物理的近期研究相呼应。

Conclusion: 量子相空间几何为理解量子引力、宇宙学和时空量子结构起源提供了新视角。研究结果表明量子相空间几何直接连接了弯曲时空几何和弯曲动量空间结构，支持玻恩互易原理的坐标-动量对偶性，并为量子相空间与中微子物理的联系提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The relativistic quantum phase space (QPS) formalism extends classical phase space by incorporating both mean values and variance-covariance matrices of quantum states, thereby providing a unified setting where the uncertainty principle and relativistic covariance coexist. In this work we explore the basic geometric structure of the QPS for the signature \((1,4)\). We construct a scalar invariant built from the mean values and the inverse variance-covariance matrix, and prove its invariance under linear canonical transformations. For quantum states that saturate the uncertainty relations, and define the QPS itself, the invariant takes a value that encodes two fundamental length scales: a large scale characterising maximal coordinate uncertainties and a small scale characterising minimal coordinate uncertainties. From this invariance we derive a geometric equation that unifies the mean values and the quantum fluctuations. Analysing two asymptotic regimes reveals two physically significant limits: one leads to a curved spacetime geometry, consistent with current cosmological observations; the other yields a curved momenta space structure. These limits suggest a direct connection between quantum phase space geometry, cosmology, and quantum gravity, offering new perspectives on the origin of the quantum structure of spacetime. The results also resonate with the principle of Born reciprocity, which posits a fundamental duality between coordinates and momenta, and align with recent works on the relation between QPS and neutrino physics.

</details>


### [89] [Oxide-nitride heteroepitaxy for low-loss dielectrics in superconducting quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29065)
*David A. Garcia-Wetten,Mitchell J. Walker,Peter G. Lim,André Vallières,Maria G. Jimenez-Guillermo,Miguel A. Alvarado,Dominic P. Goronzy,Anna Grassellino,Jens Koch,Vinayak P. Dravid,Mark C. Hersam,Michael J. Bedzyk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文报道了在超导量子电路中集成单晶γ-Al₂O₃介电层，首次直接测量其低本征微波损耗，为紧凑型量子器件提供了有前景的材料平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前超导量子比特技术受限于非晶态介电材料的高微波损耗，需要寻找具有低本征损耗的高度结晶、化学计量比精确的介电材料，但实验验证的平台仍然稀缺。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积技术生长异质外延TiN/γ-Al₂O₃/TiN三层结构，通过高分辨率成像、衍射和光谱测量验证各层单晶质量和化学完整性，使用微波集总元件谐振器测量介电损耗。

Result: 成功制备了单晶质量好、缺陷少、界面阴离子扩散有限的异质外延结构，首次直接测量到γ-Al₂O₃的低本征双能级系统损耗δ_TLS⁰ = (2.8 ± 0.1) × 10⁻⁵。

Conclusion: 过渡金属氮化物上的异质外延氧化物成为超导量子电路的有吸引力材料平台，特别适用于紧凑型器件架构如合并元件transmon和微波动力学电感探测器。

Abstract: Superconducting qubits show great promise for the realization of fault-tolerant quantum computing, but lossy, amorphous dielectrics limit current technology. Identifying highly crystalline and stoichiometric dielectrics with intrinsically low microwave loss is therefore a central materials challenge, yet experimentally validated platforms remain scarce. In this work, we integrate a crystalline dielectric into a heteroepitaxial TiN/$γ$-Al$_2$O$_3$/TiN trilayer grown via pulsed laser deposition. Correlative high-resolution imaging, diffraction, and spectroscopy measurements confirm the single-crystal quality and chemical integrity of all layers, with minimal defects and limited anion interdiffusion across the oxide-nitride interfaces. Using microwave lumped-element resonators with parallel-plate capacitors, we report the first direct measurement of the dielectric loss of epitaxial $γ$-Al$_2$O$_3$, for which we find a low intrinsic two-level system loss, $δ_{\text{TLS}}^0 = (2.8 \pm 0.1) \times 10^{-5}$. These results establish heteroepitaxial oxides on transition metal nitrides as an attractive materials platform for superconducting quantum circuits, particularly for integration into compact device architectures such as merged-element transmons and microwave kinetic inductance detectors.

</details>

*Ryan Babbush,Adam Zalcman,Craig Gidney,Michael Broughton,Tanuj Khattar,Hartmut Neven,Thiago Bergamaschi,Justin Drake,Dan Boneh*

TL;DR: 该白皮书分析了量子计算架构对区块链安全的影响，提供了破解256位椭圆曲线离散对数问题的新资源估算，并讨论了量子攻击对加密货币的威胁及后量子密码迁移的紧迫性。

Result: 1) Shor算法破解256位椭圆曲线离散对数问题可在<1200逻辑量子比特和<9000万Toffoli门或<1450逻辑量子比特和<7000万Toffoli门配置下执行；2) 在超导架构上，这些电路可在几分钟内使用少于50万物理量子比特执行；3) 快时钟CRQCs将能够对某些加密货币的公共内存池交易实施即时攻击；4) 识别了智能合约、权益证明共识、数据可用性采样等高级功能带来的系统性风险。

Conclusion: 量子计算对区块链安全构成实质性威胁，特别是快时钟量子架构。需要技术解决方案与公共政策框架相结合，包括数字资产回收监管机制。所有脆弱的加密货币社区应立即加入向后量子密码的迁移进程。

Abstract: This whitepaper seeks to elucidate implications that the capabilities of developing quantum architectures have on blockchain vulnerabilities and mitigation strategies. First, we provide new resource estimates for breaking the 256-bit Elliptic Curve Discrete Logarithm Problem, the core of modern blockchain cryptography. We demonstrate that Shor's algorithm for this problem can execute with either <1200 logical qubits and <90 million Toffoli gates or <1450 logical qubits and <70 million Toffoli gates. In the interest of responsible disclosure, we use a zero-knowledge proof to validate these results without disclosing attack vectors. On superconducting architectures with 1e-3 physical error rates and planar connectivity, those circuits can execute in minutes using fewer than half a million physical qubits. We introduce a critical distinction between fast-clock (such as superconducting and photonic) and slow-clock (such as neutral atom and ion trap) architectures. Our analysis reveals that the first fast-clock CRQCs would enable on-spend attacks on public mempool transactions of some cryptocurrencies. We survey major cryptocurrency vulnerabilities through this lens, identifying systemic risks associated with advanced features in some blockchains such as smart contracts, Proof-of-Stake consensus, and Data Availability Sampling, as well as the enduring concern of abandoned assets. We argue that technical solutions would benefit from accompanying public policy and discuss various frameworks of digital salvage to regulate the recovery or destruction of dormant assets while preventing adversarial seizure. We also discuss implications for other digital assets and tokenization as well as challenges and successful examples of the ongoing transition to Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC). Finally, we urge all vulnerable cryptocurrency communities to join the ongoing migration to PQC without delay.

</details>


### [91] [Color code off-the-hook: avoiding hook errors with a single auxiliary per plaquette](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28852)
*Gilad Kishony,Austin Fowler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于平面颜色码的新型单辅助量子比特综合征提取电路，通过颜色相关的门调度避免恶性钩状错误，保持完整的电路级距离，并具有最小深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面颜色码中的综合征提取面临高权重稳定子和钩状错误的挑战，这些错误会降低电路级距离。现有使用单辅助量子比特的电路通常会使电路级距离减半，需要改进。

Method: 设计了一种单辅助量子比特的综合征提取电路，采用颜色相关的门调度方案，避免所有体区内的恶性钩状错误。电路具有最小深度：所有相同泡利类型的稳定子在六个时间步内并行测量。该调度也可应用于XYZ颜色码电路。

Result: 在体区内完全避免了恶性钩状错误，保持了完整的电路级距离。在边界处，单个钩状错误不会降低距离，只有特定组合的钩状错误（称为分数钩状错误）才会影响。蒙特卡洛模拟显示，在各种电路级噪声模型和物理错误率下，该电路性能优于现有技术。

Conclusion: 提出的颜色相关门调度方案有效解决了平面颜色码中钩状错误导致的电路级距离降低问题，实现了高性能的综合征提取电路，为量子纠错提供了改进方案。

Abstract: Syndrome extraction in the planar color code is complicated by high weight stabilizers and hook errors that can reduce the circuit-level distance. With a single auxiliary qubit per plaquette, any spatially uniform circuit halves the circuit-level distance. We propose a single-auxiliary syndrome extraction circuit with color-dependent gate schedules that avoids all malign hook errors in the bulk, thereby preserving the full circuit-level distance. The circuit has minimal depth: all stabilizers of the same Pauli type are measured in parallel in six time steps. Furthermore, this schedule can be readily applied to the XYZ color code circuit, yielding an improved temporal distance. We find that at the boundary, no single hook error alone reduces the distance; instead, only certain combinations of hook errors do, which we call fractional hook errors. We demonstrate through Monte Carlo simulations over a range of circuit-level noise models and physical error rates that our circuit outperforms the previous state of the art.

</details>

Result: 理论上声称能在多项式时间内解决NP完全问题，但前提是多世界解释是正确的，且需要承担宇宙尺度的风险

Conclusion: 这是一个思想实验式的理论探讨，提出了量子计算解决NP问题的极端可能性，但实际可行性依赖于量子多世界解释的正确性和可操作性

Abstract: In this paper we propose a totally serious algorithm to solve NP problems in polynomial time provided one is willing to wager the fate of all observers in the universe on the many-world interpretation of quantum theory being correct.
