### [93] [Non-stabilizerness and U(1) symmetry in chaotic many-body quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28870)
*Daniele Iannotti,Angelo Russotto,Barbara Jasser,Jovan Odavić,Alioscia Hamma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究U(1)对称性约束下随机纯态的非稳定子性，发现守恒荷显著抑制了magic，且magic对电荷密度涨落比纠缠熵更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对称性约束对量子态非稳定子性（magic）的影响，比较对称性约束下magic与纠缠熵的不同响应行为，理解相互作用局域性在其中的作用。

Method: 使用稳定子熵作为非稳定子性度量，推导U(1)约束下Haar随机态的平均值和方差；在热力学极限下分析标度行为；用两个混沌多体系统（cSYK模型和XXZ链）的中间谱本征态验证理论预测。

Result: 守恒荷导致非稳定子性显著抑制；magic对电荷密度涨落比纠缠熵更鲁棒；cSYK模型与理论预测高度一致，局域XXZ链存在系统偏差，表明相互作用局域性起重要作用。

Conclusion: 对称性约束显著影响量子态的非稳定子性，magic与纠缠对对称性约束的响应存在本质差异，相互作用局域性是决定实际系统与随机态理论预测一致性的关键因素。

Abstract: We present exact, closed-form results for the non-stabilizerness of random pure states subject to a U(1) symmetry constraint. Using stabilizer entropy as our non-stabilizerness monotone, we derive the average and the variance for U(1)-constrained Haar random states. We show that the presence of a conserved charge leads to a substantial suppression of non-stabilizerness (magic) compared to the unconstrained case, and identify a qualitative difference between entanglement and magic response. In the thermodynamic limit, stabilizer entropy exhibits a different leading-order scaling close to a vanishing relative charge density, implying that magic is more robust to charge density fluctuations than entanglement entropy. We test our analytical predictions against midspectrum eigenstates of two chaotic many-body systems with conserved $U(1)$ charge: the complex-fermion Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (cSYK) model and a Heisenberg XXZ chain with next-to-nearest-neighbour couplings and conserved magnetization. We find an excellent agreement for the non-local cSYK model and systematic deviations for the local XXZ chain, highlighting the role of interaction locality.

</details>


### [94] [Quantum Approximate Optimization of Integer Graph Problems and Surpassing Semidefinite Programming for Max-k-Cut](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05956)
*Anuj Apte,Sami Boulebnane,Yuwei Jin,Sivaprasad Omanakuttan,Michael A. Perlin,Ruslan Shaydulin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了量子近似优化算法（QAOA）在整数优化问题上的应用，特别是Max-k-Cut问题，发现QAOA在某些参数范围内能超越经典半定规划算法，为量子优势开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法在二进制优化问题上已有广泛研究，但在整数优化问题上的应用相对较少。本文旨在探索QAOA在整数优化问题上的潜力，特别是使用qudit编码整数变量，以寻找量子优势的新途径。

Method: 1. 将QAOA应用于图上的整数问题，使用qudit编码整数变量
2. 推导出深度-p QAOA在高围长d-正则图上的通用迭代公式
3. 公式计算成本与QAOA深度p呈指数关系，但与图大小无关
4. 针对Max-k-Cut问题评估公式（p≤4）
5. 引入基于饱和度（degree-of-saturation）的新启发式算法作为更强的经典基线

Result: 1. 在特定参数范围内（k=3且d≤10，k=4且d≤40），QAOA的表现优于Frieze-Jerrum半定规划算法
2. 新提出的基于饱和度的启发式算法在经验上优于Frieze-Jerrum算法和浅层QAOA
3. 数值证据表明QAOA在深度p≤20时可能超越该启发式算法

Conclusion: 超越二进制优化问题转向整数优化问题可以为量子优势开辟新的途径。虽然浅层QAOA在某些情况下已被经典算法超越，但深度QAOA（p≤20）显示出超越经典启发式算法的潜力，表明量子算法在整数优化领域具有应用前景。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms for binary optimization problems have been the subject of extensive study. However, the application of quantum algorithms to integer optimization problems remains comparatively unexplored. In this paper, we study the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) applied to integer problems on graphs, with each integer variable encoded in a qudit. We derive a general iterative formula for depth-$p$ QAOA expectation on high-girth $d$-regular graphs of arbitrary size. The cost of evaluating the formula is exponential in the QAOA depth $p$ but does not depend on the graph size. Evaluating this formula for Max-$k$-Cut problem for $p\leq 4$, we identify parameter regimes ($k=3$ with degree $d \leq 10$ and $k=4$ with $d \leq 40$) in which QAOA outperforms the Frieze-Jerrum semi-definite programming (SDP) algorithm, which provides the best worst-case guarantee on the approximation ratio. To strengthen the classical baseline we introduce a new heuristic algorithm, based on the degree-of-saturation, that empirically outperforms both the Frieze-Jerrum algorithm and shallow-depth QAOA. Nevertheless, we provide numerical evidence that QAOA may overtake this heuristic at depth $p\leq 20$. Our results show that moving beyond binary to integer optimization problems can open up new avenues for quantum advantage.

</details>


### [95] [Real Variance-Based Variational Quantum Eigensolver for Non-Hermitian Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28892)
*Durgesh Pandey,Ankit Kumar Das,P. Arumugam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于实方差变分量子本征求解器（RVVQE）的非厄米算符系统化算法，使用厄米测量确保收敛到真实本征态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米算符在开放量子系统中自然出现，具有共振和衰变等特征，其本征值为复数。传统量子算法（如VQE）针对厄米算符设计，无法有效恢复非厄米矩阵的正确本征值。

Method: 基于实方差变分量子本征求解器（RVVQE）的系统化公式，识别能保证收敛到真实本征态的正确代价函数，仅使用厄米测量实现算法

Result: 通过数值结果和计算指标展示了该算法在维度递增的密集非厄米矩阵层次结构上的性能和可扩展性

Conclusion: RVVQE为处理非厄米算符提供了一种有效的量子算法框架，仅需厄米测量即可实现，具有实际可部署性

Abstract: Non-Hermitian operators naturally arise in the description of open quantum systems, which exhibit features such as resonances and decay processes, where the associated eigenvalues are complex. Standard quantum algorithms, including the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE), are designed for Hermitian operators and are ineffective in recovering correct eigenvalues for non-Hermitian matrices. We present a systematic formulation based on a Real Variance-based Variational Quantum Eigensolver (RVVQE) for non-Hermitian operators. A correct cost function that guarantees convergence to the true eigenstates is identified. Our implementation utilizes Hermitian measurements only, rendering the algorithm easily deliverable. The performance and scalability of the proposed algorithm on a hierarchy of dense non-Hermitian matrices of increasing dimension are demonstrated with numerical results and computational metrics.

</details>


### [96] [Iterative Optimization with Partial Convergence Guarantees on Neutral Atom Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28933)
*Cédrick Perron,Yves Bérubé-Lauzière,Victor Drouin-Touchette*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Lp-Quts：一种混合量子-经典框架，将中性原子量子计算机采样器集成到经典割平面算法中，用于解决最大加权独立集问题，在保证理论收敛性的同时减少量子资源需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子计算机在解决最大加权独立集问题时面临两个关键限制：原子布局对可实现图几何的约束，以及缺乏性能保证。需要一种方法既能利用量子采样器的优势，又能克服这些限制。

Method: 提出Lp-Quts混合框架，将NAQC采样器集成到经典割平面算法中。每轮迭代中：1) 通过松弛线性规划获得MWIS边界并诱导出简化图；2) 使用模拟量子采样器从简化图中采样独立集；3) 通过新颖的样本信息分离问题指导奇环割选择，加速收敛。

Result: 在最多300个顶点的实例上评估，Lp-Quts在相同采样预算下达到最优解的5-10%范围内，优于直接模拟量子协议和贪心基线。对于t-完美图，继承了割平面理论的多项式时间收敛保证。模拟退火在此规模下仍是最强的基于采样的求解器。

Conclusion: 量子采样器可以有效地嵌入经典优化框架中，在减少量子资源的同时提供接近最优的解决方案，并保留形式化保证。这展示了混合量子-经典方法在解决组合优化问题中的潜力。

Abstract: Neutral atom quantum computers (NAQCs) have emerged as a promising platform for solving the maximum weighted independent set (MWIS) problem. However, analog quantum approaches face two key limitations: constraints of the atomic layout on realizable graph geometries and the absence of performance guarantees. We introduce Lp-Quts, a hybrid quantum-classical framework that integrates an NAQC sampler into a classical cutting-plane algorithm. At each iteration, a relaxed linear program (RLP) bounds the MWIS and induces a reduced graph from which independent sets are sampled using an analog quantum sampler. A novel sample-informed separation problem guides odd-cycle cut selection and accelerates convergence. For t-perfect graphs, Lp-Quts inherits polynomial-time convergence guarantees from the classical theory of cutting planes. We evaluate our approach on instances with up to 300 vertices -- a scale that exceeds the capabilities of current NAQC hardware. In this regime, Lp-Quts reaches solutions within 5--10\% of optimality, outperforming direct analog quantum protocols and greedy baselines under equal sampling budgets. As expected, simulated annealing remains the strongest sample-based solver at this scale. These results demonstrate how quantum samplers can be effectively embedded within classical optimization frameworks to deliver near-optimal solutions with reduced quantum resources while preserving formal guarantees.

</details>


### [97] [Bell's Inequality, Causal Bounds, and Quantum Bayesian Computation: A Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28973)
*Nick Polson,Vadim Sokolov,Daniel Zantedeschi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文揭示了贝尔不等式与因果推断中工具变量模型之间的深刻对应关系，两者共享相同的边际兼容性多面体结构，为量子信息论、因果计量经济学和贝叶斯计算建立了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上贝尔不等式被视为量子力学特有的非经典现象，而因果推断中的工具变量模型则属于经典统计范畴。论文旨在揭示这两个看似不同领域之间的深层数学结构对应关系，建立统一的框架来理解非定域性、因果推断和计算复杂性之间的联系。

Method: 通过分析边际兼容性多面体的几何结构，证明贝尔局部隐变量模型与因果推断中的工具变量模型在数学上同构。将工具变量类比为测量设置，潜在混杂因子类比为隐变量λ。进一步将对应关系扩展到算法（Kolmogorov复杂度）和熵形式的贝尔不等式、NPA半定规划层次结构以及MIP*=RE不可判定性结果。

Result: 建立了贝尔不等式与工具变量不等式、Balke-Pearl线性规划边界、Tian-Pearl因果概率之间的精确对应关系，它们都是同一边际兼容性多面体的不同面。证明了Fine定理（CHSH不等式成立当且仅当存在联合分布）正是这一对应的关键枢纽。揭示了Born规则/Bayes规则对偶性利用与贝尔违反相同的非交换性，为后验推断提供多项式加速。

Conclusion: 论文建立了量子信息论、因果计量经济学和贝叶斯计算之间的具体对应词典。这一框架不仅统一了不同领域的数学结构，还为量子因果推断算法和函数近似的量子架构等新研究方向提供了理论基础，表明量子非定域性与经典因果推断之间存在深刻的数学同构关系。

Abstract: Bell inequalities characterize the boundary of the local-realist correlation polytope -- the set of joint probability distributions achievable by classical hidden-variable models. Quantum mechanics exceeds this boundary through non-commutativity, reaching the Tsirelson bound $2\sqrt{2}$ for CHSH. We show that this polytope structure is not specific to quantum foundations: it appears identically in the causal inference literature, where the instrumental inequality, the Balke--Pearl linear programming bounds, and the Tian--Pearl probabilities of causation all arise as facets of the same marginal compatibility polytope. Fine's theorem -- that CHSH inequalities hold if and only if a joint distribution exists -- is precisely the pivot: the instrumental variable model in causal inference is structurally equivalent to the Bell local hidden-variable model, with the instrument playing the role of the measurement setting and the latent confounder playing the role of the hidden variable $λ$. We develop this correspondence in detail, extending it to algorithmic (Kolmogorov complexity) and entropic formulations of Bell inequalities, the NPA semidefinite programming hierarchy, and the MIP$^*$=RE undecidability result. We further show that the Born-rule / Bayes-rule duality underlying quantum Bayesian computation exploits the same non-commutativity that enables Bell violation, providing polynomial speedups for posterior inference. The framework yields a concrete dictionary between quantum information theory, causal econometrics, and Bayesian computation, and suggests new directions including NPA-based quantum causal inference algorithms and quantum architectures for function approximation.

</details>


### [98] [Nonequilibrium phases and quantum correlations in synthetic transport models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24478)
*Uddhav Sen,Federico Carollo,Sascha Wald*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子元胞自动机在具备中途测量和重置能力的量子设备上实现，用于研究经典和量子输运的最小模型，发现纠缠主导瞬态演化而稳态可保留超越纠缠的量子关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用具备中途测量和重置能力的量子设备（如量子计算机和双物种里德堡量子模拟器）实现量子元胞自动机，研究如何在这些系统中实现经典和量子输运的最小模型，探索相干动力学贡献如何导致量子效应和相关性的涌现。

Method: 采用量子元胞自动机框架，通过局部更新实现的幺正门进行离散时间演化，实现封闭和合成开放动力学。具体研究离散时间完全不对称简单排斥过程，分析相干动力学贡献对量子效应和相关性形成的影响。

Result: 发现二分纠缠主导系统的瞬态演化，而稳态可以保留超越纠缠的量子关联。相干动力学贡献允许量子效应和相关性的涌现，为在量子设备上实现输运模型提供了可行途径。

Conclusion: 量子元胞自动机为在量子设备上实现输运模型提供了可行框架，能够表征强驱动系统中的集体量子关联，为研究量子输运现象开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum devices featuring mid-circuit measurement and reset capabilities, such as quantum computers and dual-species Rydberg quantum simulators, enable the realization of quantum cellular automata. These systems evolve in discrete time following local updates implemented by unitary gates, and allow for the realization of both closed and synthetic open dynamics. Here, we focus on quantum cellular automata that implement minimal models of classical and quantum transport. To illustrate our ideas, we focus on a discrete-time totally asymmetric simple exclusion process and investigate how coherent dynamical contributions allow for the emergence of quantum effects and correlations. We find that bipartite entanglement dominates the transient evolution, while stationary states can retain quantum correlations beyond entanglement. Our results suggest viable routes for realizing transport models on quantum devices and characterizing collective quantum correlations in strongly driven systems.

</details>


### [99] [Can Quantum Field Theory be Recovered from Time-Symmetric Stochastic Mechanics? Part II: Prospects for a Trajectory Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28983)
*Simon Friederich,Mritunjay Tyagi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了将量子场论中的Husimi Q函数演化方程解释为时间对称随机过程轨迹的可能性，发现虽然存在轨迹解释，但存在重要限制，且动力学本质上是非马尔可夫的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先前工作中，作者推导出了时间反演不变的随机Liouville方程，并证明其与玻色子量子场论中Husimi Q函数的演化方程一致。本文旨在研究将该演化方程解释为底层随机轨迹的可能性，探索将量子场论理解为时间对称随机过程统计力学的项目。

Method: 基于Drummond的时间对称随机作用形式体系，证明无迹扩散Fokker-Planck方程定义了在混合时间边界条件下随机轨迹的条件概率测度。分析该轨迹解释对任意量子态的适用性限制，并研究动力学的马尔可夫性质。

Result: 发现虽然存在轨迹解释，但存在重要缺口：尚未证明每个Q函数都能表示为这些边界值条件概率的加权平均。轨迹解释适用于固定边界条件的系综，但不能直接推广到任意量子态。同时证明Drummond的轨迹动力学本质上是非马尔可夫的，这是随机性与时间反演不变性结合的自然结果。

Conclusion: 非马尔可夫性使该动力学超出了本体模型框架的范围，从而解释了为什么主要的隐变量理论不可行定理不排除该方法。这些结果澄清了将量子场论理解为时间对称随机过程统计力学这一项目的成就和剩余挑战。

Abstract: In a companion paper we derived a unique time-reversal-invariant stochastic generalization of the Liouville equation and showed that it coincides with the evolution equation for the Husimi $Q$-function in a broad class of bosonic quantum field theories. Here we investigate the prospects for interpreting that evolution equation in terms of underlying stochastic trajectories. Drawing on Drummond's time-symmetric stochastic action formalism, we show that the traceless diffusion Fokker-Planck equation defines a natural measure over stochastic trajectories conditional on mixed-time boundary conditions. However, we identify a significant gap: it has not been established that every $Q$-function can be represented as a weighted average of these conditional probabilities over boundary values. The trajectory interpretation holds for ensembles with fixed boundary conditions but does not straightforwardly extend to arbitrary quantum states. Despite this limitation, we show that Drummond's trajectory dynamics are fundamentally non-Markovian -- a natural consequence of combining stochasticity with time-reversal invariance. This non-Markovianity places the dynamics outside the scope of the ontological models framework and thereby explains why the major no-go theorems for hidden-variable theories do not rule out the approach. These results clarify both the achievements and the remaining challenges in the project of understanding quantum field theory as the statistical mechanics of time-symmetric stochastic processes.

</details>


### [100] [Scalable phonon-laser arrays with self-organized synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29099)
*Hugo Molinares,Guillermo Romero,Victor Montenegro,Vitalie Eremeev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于局域驱动的可扩展、可寻址的声子激光阵列方案，解决了现有声子激光系统缺乏可扩展性和灵活性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前声子激光方案面临两个关键限制：缺乏可扩展性，以及依赖所有振荡器与共同场的耦合，这显著限制了灵活性并阻碍了在特定位置按需产生声子激光。考虑到许多应用和理论见解自然产生于可扩展的多体系统，解决这些限制具有及时性。

Method: 通过局域驱动在量子多体伊辛型自旋链中生成可扩展的、可单独寻址的声子激光阵列。该方法采用纯局域驱动而非共同耦合总线，建立了机械振荡器从热运动转变为持续相干自振荡的共振条件。通过简单地开关自旋-机械耦合相互作用，可以在满足特定共振条件时按需在特定位置激发单个机械振荡器。

Result: 该声子激光阵列对共振失配具有鲁棒性，在共振附近自然表现出成对自组织同步和全局锁相。方案提供了模块化和可扩展的架构，非常适合集成到大规模量子系统中。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了如何通过局域驱动在量子多体系统中实现可扩展的、可寻址的声子激光阵列，解决了现有方案的局限性，并提出了在当前实验能力范围内的实验实施方案。

Abstract: Quantum mechanical oscillators operating at frequencies up to the GHz regime have been predicted to support phonon lasing -- self-sustained coherent vibrational motion emerging when the effective gain exceeds intrinsic losses. Current phonon-laser proposals face two key limitations, namely: they lack scalability and rely on coupling all oscillators to a common field, which significantly restricts flexibility and prevents selective, on-demand phonon lasing at specific locations. Given that numerous applications and theoretical insights naturally emerge from scalable many-body systems, addressing these limitations is timely. In this Letter, we demonstrate how scalable arrays of individually addressable phonon lasers can be generated through local driving in a quantum many-body Ising-like spin chain. We rigorously establish the resonance conditions under which mechanical oscillators transition from thermal motion to sustained coherent self-oscillation. Unlike previous approaches that rely on a common coupling bus, our proposal employs purely local driving, resulting in an inherently modular and scalable architecture ideally suited for integration into large-scale quantum systems. Additionally, our approach enables on-demand lasing of individual mechanical oscillators at specific sites by simply switching the spin-mechanical coupling interaction on and off, provided specific resonance conditions are satisfied. Notably, our phonon laser array is robust against resonance mismatches and naturally exhibits both pairwise self-organized synchronization and global phase locking near resonance. Finally, we outline an experimental implementation within current experimental capabilities.

</details>


### [101] [High-efficiency and noise-immune quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29175)
*Guohui Dong,Mengqi Yu,Yao Yao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过动态调制提升量子电池充电效率和噪声免疫性，有效抑制超强耦合区的反旋转项，实现高效充电和完美能量存储


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池在超强耦合区虽然能通过纠缠增强充电功率，但反旋转项会诱导能量基的相干性，显著降低充电效率，同时量子电池对退相干效应脆弱

Method: 提出基于动态调制的高效噪声免疫量子电池方案，通过时变调制有效减少反旋转耦合，优化调制参数可完全消除反旋转相互作用，使Dicke量子电池达到最优充电

Result: 动态调制显著提高充电效率，在适当参数下Dicke量子电池能像Tavis-Cummings量子电池一样最优充电；存储过程中对纯退相和耗散噪声都具有高度鲁棒性，通过有效浴工程可实现完美能量存储

Conclusion: 该方案为强大量子电池的实现提供了坚实基础，有望显著促进未来能量存储和传输技术的发展，且在当前实验平台上具有可行性

Abstract: Nowadays, quantum batteries (QBs) have been designed to outperform their classical counterparts by leveraging quantum advantages. For instance, the charging power greatly benefits from the entanglement generation of a collective charging scheme (e.g., the Dicke QB), especially in the ultrastrong coupling (USC) regime or even larger. However, apart from the fragility of the QB under intrinsic decoherence effects, another critical drawback emerges inevitably. Specifically, the non-negligible counter-rotating (CR) term in the USC regime would induce coherence in the energy basis of QB, thus remarkably degrading the charging efficiency. To tackle these challenges, we propose a high-efficiency and noise-immune QB boosted by dynamical modulation. It is demonstrated that the time-varying modulation can effectively reduce the CR coupling, resulting in a notable improvement in charging efficiency. Particularly, for a judicious choice of modulation parameters that entirely eliminate the CR interaction, the Dicke QB can be charged optimally, resembling the behavior of the Tavis-Cummings QB. In the subsequent storage process, beyond the natural robustness to pure dephasing noise, our scenario is also highly resilient to the dissipation noise and thus can achieve perfect energy storage by effective bath engineering. While feasible with current experimental platforms, our proposal offers a solid foundation for the implementation of a powerful QB and may drastically promote the development of energy storage and delivery techniques in the future.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum heat transport in nonequilibrium anisotropic Dicke model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29180)
*Kong Junran,Mao Mang,Liu Huan,Wang Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了非平衡各向异性Dicke模型中的热输运，发现各向异性光-物质相互作用对稳态热流具有重要调制作用，强耦合时热流被显著抑制，中等耦合时热流被增强，并分析了热整流效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索各向异性光-物质相互作用对量子输运的影响，特别是对非平衡热输运的调制机制，以理解量子热器件（如热阀和热二极管）的物理基础。

Method: 采用非平衡各向异性Dicke模型，其中量子比特集合与各向异性光子场集体相互作用，各组分与玻色热库耦合。使用量子化主方程（DME）研究耗散动力学，在约化各向异性Dicke系统的本征基中处理强量子比特-光子耦合。

Result: 各向异性量子比特-光子相互作用对稳态热流调制至关重要：强耦合时大各向异性因子显著抑制热流，中等耦合时增强热流。增加量子比特数量放大流动特征（峰值增加、谷值降低）。在热力学极限近似下推导出两个解析热流表达式，显示共隧穿热输运图像，并作为有限尺寸模型的边界。还分析了热整流效应。

Conclusion: 各向异性光-物质相互作用是调控量子热输运的关键参数，能够显著改变热流特性，为设计基于各向异性相互作用的量子热器件提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium heat transport and quantum thermodynamics in light-matter interacting systems have received increasing attention. Quantum thermal devices, e.g., heat valve and head diode, have been realized. Recently, it has been discovered that the anisotropic light-matter interactions can greatly modify the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of hybrid quantum systems, leading to nontrivial quantum phase transitions, quantum metrology, and nonclassicality of photons. To explore the influences of anisotropic light-matter interactions on quantum transport, we investigate heat flow in the nonequilibrium anisotropic Dicke model. In this model, an ensemble of qubits collectively interacts with an anisotropic photon field. Each component interacts with bosonic thermal reservoirs. Quantum dressed master equation (DME) is included to properly study dissipative dynamics of the anisotropic Dicke model. Within the eigenbasis of the reduced anisotropic Dicke system, strong qubit-photon couplings can be properly handled. Our results demonstrate that anisotropic qubit-photon interactions are crucial for modulating steady-state heat flow. In particular, it is found that under strong coupling the heat flow is dramatically suppressed by a large anisotropic qubit-photon factor. While under moderate coupling, the anisotropic qubit-photon interactions enhance the heat flow. Moreover, the increase in the number of qubits amplifies the flow characteristics, with the peaks increasing and the valleys decreasing. Besides, we derive two analytical expressions of heat flows in thermodynamic limit approximation with limiting anisotropic factors. These heat currents exhibit the cotunneling heat transport pictures. They also serve as the upper boundaries for the heat flows in the finite-size anisotropic Dicke model. We also analyze the thermal rectification effect in the anisotropic Dicke model.

</details>


### [103] [Calculating the quantum Fisher information via the truncated Wigner method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29196)
*Thakur G. M. Hiranandani,Joseph J. Hope,Simon A. Haine*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于随机采样量子相空间模拟的参数估计新方法，通过截断维格纳近似从半经典随机样本计算量子费希尔信息


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展能够高效计算基本灵敏度极限的量子系统类别，使更多具有计量学应用价值的量子态分析成为可能

Method: 使用截断维格纳近似进行随机采样量子相空间模拟，从半经典随机样本计算量子费希尔信息

Result: 该方法能够计算超出自旋压缩区域的系统量子费希尔信息，其中矩方法失效

Conclusion: 提出的方法扩展了可高效分析量子计量学极限的系统范围，为更广泛的量子态灵敏度分析提供了有效工具

Abstract: In this work, we propose new methods of parameter estimation using stochastic sampling quantum phase-space simulations. We show that it is possible to compute the quantum Fisher information (QFI) from semiclassical stochastic samples using the Truncated Wigner Approximation (TWA). This method extends the class of quantum systems whose fundamental sensitivity limit can be computed efficiently to any system that can be modelled using the TWA, allowing the analysis of more meteorologically useful quantum states. We illustrate this approach with examples, including a system that evolves outside the spin-squeezing regime, where the method of moments fails.

</details>


### [104] [Direct measurement of the energy spectrum of a quantum dot qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29229)
*J. Reily,Daniel J. King,Jonathan C. Marcks,M. A. Wolfe,Piotr Marciniec,E. S. Joseph,Tyler J. Kovach,Brighton X. Coe,Mark Friesen,Benjamin D. Woods,M. A. Eriksson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种名为DAXS的哈密顿量无关技术，用于在宽能量范围内测量双量子点能谱，并应用于Si/SiGe双量子点系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术（如DAPS）主要提供能级细节，但对隧道耦合的估计通常只能揭示哈密顿量的一小部分，需要更全面的测量方法

Method: 开发了delta轴光谱学（DAXS）技术，能够在每个失谐值下测量双量子点在宽能量范围内的能谱

Result: 将DAXS应用于Si/SiGe双量子点，提取了15级类Hubbard哈密顿量的对角和非对角耦合参数，与实验测量结果高度一致

Conclusion: DAXS技术能够全面测量双量子点能谱，为理解和操作自旋量子比特提供了更完整的哈密顿量参数映射

Abstract: The mapping between gate voltages applied to a double quantum dot, and the parameters of a Hubbard-like Hamiltonian, is of utmost importance for understanding and operating spin qubits. State-of-the-art techniques for measuring Hamiltonian parameters (e.g., detuning axis pulsed spectroscopy, DAPS) provide details about energy levels; however, tunnel coupling estimates typically reveal only a small portion of the full Hamiltonian. Here, we demonstrate a Hamiltonian-agnostic technique for measuring the double dot energy spectrum over a wide energy range, at every value of the detuning, called delta-axis spectroscopy (DAXS). We apply the DAXS method to obtain the energy spectrum of a Si/SiGe double quantum dot and use this data to extract the diagonal and off-diagonal couplings of a 15-level Hubbard-like Hamiltonian, demonstrating very good agreement with the experimental measurements.

</details>


### [105] [From Promises to Totality: A Framework for Ruling Out Quantum Speedups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29256)
*Thomas Huffstutler,Upendra Kapshikar,David Miloschewsky,Supartha Podder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了部分布尔函数何时能（或不能）展现超多项式量子查询加速，提出了通过承诺感知复杂度度量和函数完备化两种互补视角来排除此类加速的通用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解部分布尔函数中量子查询复杂度相对于经典查询复杂度的超多项式加速现象，并开发系统性的理论框架来识别和排除这类加速的可能性。

Method: 1. 引入承诺版本的组合度量（如块敏感度及其变体）；2. 证明当相关承诺和完备化度量坍缩时，确定性和量子查询复杂度必然多项式相关；3. 分析结构化承诺族（对称部分函数和汉明切片支持的承诺）；4. 形式化完备化复杂度作为部分函数在所有总完备化上度量的最小值；5. 应用该框架推导具有良好完备化的函数类的非加速准则。

Result: 1. 建立了承诺和完备化度量坍缩与多项式相关性的理论联系；2. 对对称部分函数获得了以单一间隙参数为特征的尖锐刻画（至多项式因子）；3. 对k-切片域获得了精细的切片依赖界；4. 证明了完备化度量的可完备性捕捉了超多项式量子加速的可能性；5. 推导了具有低最大影响函数和高效可识别域函数类的广泛非加速准则。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解部分布尔函数中量子查询加速现象提供了系统框架，通过承诺感知度量和函数完备化两种互补视角，能够有效识别和排除超多项式量子加速的可能性，并对特定函数类建立了精确的刻画和边界。

Abstract: We study when partial Boolean functions can (and cannot) exhibit superpolynomial quantum query speedups, and develop a general framework for ruling out such speedups via two complementary lenses: promise-aware complexity measures and function completions.
  First, we introduce promise versions of standard combinatorial measures (including block sensitivity and related variants) and prove that if the relevant promise and completion measures collapse, then deterministic and quantum query complexities are necessarily polynomially related, i.e., $D(f)=poly(Q(f))$. We then analyze structured families of promises, including symmetric partial functions and promises supported on Hamming slices, obtaining sharp (up to polynomial factors) characterizations in terms of a single gap parameter for the symmetric case and refined slice-dependent bounds for $k$-slice domains.
  Next, we formalize completion complexity as the minimum of a measure over total completions of a partial function, and show that completability of a measure captures the possibility of superpolynomial quantum speedups. Finally, we apply this viewpoint to derive broad non-speedup criteria for some classes of functions admitting well-behaved completions, such as functions with low maximum influence on both the standard and $p$-biased hypercubes and functions with efficiently identifiable domains, and then show some hardness results for general completion techniques.

</details>


### [106] [Change in bit-flip times of Kerr parametric oscillators caused by their interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29308)
*Yuya Kano,Yohei Kawakami,Shumpei Masuda,Tomohiro Yamaji,Aiko Yamaguchi,Tetsuro Satoh,Ayuka Morioka,Kiyotaka Endo,Yuichi Igarashi,Masayuki Shirane,Tsuyoshi Yamamoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实验研究Kerr参量振荡器（KPO）间的相互作用如何降低比特翻转时间，发现相互作用导致量子态泄漏出计算空间，使比特翻转时间降低一个数量级，并讨论了缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPO间的相互作用会导致量子态泄漏出计算子空间，引发比特翻转，这会降低保真度并对基于KPO的量子信息处理构成严重问题，因此需要研究这种退化机制。

Method: 通过实验向一个KPO注入弱微波信号来模拟来自另一个KPO的光子注入，测量比特翻转时间的变化，分析注入信号的频率和功率对翻转时间的影响。

Result: 比特翻转时间因诱导激发而降低了一个数量级，具体降低程度取决于注入信号的频率和功率。相互作用导致的量子态泄漏是比特翻转的主要原因。

Conclusion: 提出了缓解KPO间相互作用导致比特翻转时间降低的方法，包括调整泵浦频率、相干态振幅和KPO间的耦合强度，这些发现为扩展基于KPO的量子计算机提供了重要见解。

Abstract: We experimentally investigate how interactions between Kerr parametric oscillators (KPOs) degrade their bit-flip times, where a bit flip is defined as a transition between the two degenerate ground states of a KPO. Interactions between KPOs cause quantum states of KPOs to leak outside the computational subspace, leading to bit flips. Bit flips degrade fidelity and pose a significant problem for KPO-based quantum information processing. We performed an experiment in which a weak microwave signal is injected into one KPO to emulate photon injection from another KPO, and find that the bit-flip time decreases by an order of magnitude due to induced excitations, depending on the frequency and power of the injected signal. Methods to mitigate the decrease in bit-flip times caused by interactions between KPOs are discussed, including adjusting the pump frequencies, coherent-state amplitudes, and couplings between KPOs. These findings provide valuable insights for scaling up KPO-based quantum computers.

</details>


### [107] [On the Entanglement Entropy Distribution of a Hybrid Quantum Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29323)
*Jeonghyeok Park,Hyukjoon Kwon,Hyeonseok Jeong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究随机幺正门和局域测量混合量子电路中纠缠熵分布的统计矩，发现高阶矩能揭示测量诱导纠缠相变中均值无法捕捉的特征


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索混合量子电路中纠缠熵分布的高阶统计矩，以揭示测量诱导动力学中仅通过平均熵无法观测到的非平凡特征，为测量诱导纠缠相变提供更有效的诊断工具

Method: 研究随机幺正门和有限速率局域测量组成的混合量子电路，分析纠缠熵分布的高阶矩（如方差与均值之比、偏度），提出描述面积律区域测量效应的唯象模型，结合体积律区域的随机环境中定向聚合物描述

Result: 发现纠缠熵分布的高阶矩在体积律和面积律相中表现出明显不同的鲁棒行为，这些量可作为测量诱导纠缠相变的有效诊断指标；提出的唯象模型与数值模拟在整个相图中匹配良好

Conclusion: 纠缠熵分布的高阶统计矩能揭示测量诱导动力学中均值无法捕捉的关键特征，为理解混合量子电路中的纠缠相变提供了新的诊断工具和理论框架

Abstract: We investigate the distribution of entanglement entropy in hybrid quantum circuits consisting of random unitary gates and local measurements applied at a finite rate. We demonstrate that higher moments of the entanglement entropy distribution, such as a ratio between the variance and the mean and skewness, capture nontrivial features of the measurement-induced dynamics that are invisible to the mean entropy alone. We demonstrate that these quantities exhibit distinct and robust behaviors across the volume-law and area-law phases, and can serve as effective diagnostics of measurement-induced entanglement transitions. We propose a phenomenological model describing the effect of measurements in the area-law regime, which, when combined with the directed polymer in a random environment description of the volume-law phase, well matches numerical simulations across the entire phase diagram.

</details>


### [108] [Multipartite controlled-NOT gates using molecules and Rydberg atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29349)
*Yi-Han Bai,Yue Wei,Chi Zhang,Weibin Li,Xiao-Qiang Shao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于非常规里德堡泵浦机制的混合极性分子-里德堡原子系统中高保真度CNOT门实现方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合极性分子丰富的内部结构和里德堡原子强偶极-偶极相互作用，构建可扩展的量子信息处理平台

Method: 利用非常规里德堡泵浦机制，在混合极性分子-里德堡原子系统中实现二对一和一对二门配置

Result: 数值模拟显示门性能对里德堡态自发辐射具有鲁棒性；四量子比特实现中，三对一和一对三CNOT门保真度超过99%

Conclusion: 混合分子-里德堡原子架构是实现可扩展量子信息处理的有前景平台

Abstract: We propose high-fidelity controlled-NOT (CNOT) gates in a hybrid system of polar molecules and Rydberg atoms based on the unconventional Rydberg pumping mechanism. By combining the rich internal structure of polar molecules with the strong dipole-dipole interactions of Rydberg atoms, we realize both two-to-one and one-to-two gate configurations. Numerical simulations show that the gate performance is robust against spontaneous emission from Rydberg states. The approach naturally extends to larger systems, as demonstrated by four-qubit implementations achieving three-to-one and one-to-three CNOT gates with fidelities exceeding 99\%. These results highlight hybrid molecule-Rydberg atom architectures as a promising platform for scalable quantum information processing.

</details>


### [109] [YZ-plane measurement-based quantum computation: Universality and Parity Architecture implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29379)
*Jaroslav Kysela,Katharina Ludwig,Nitica Sakharwade,Anette Messinger,Wolfgang Lechner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文定义了寄存器逻辑图类，证明了在均匀确定性MBQC中，当输入与输出重合时，计算必须在YZ平面测量下进行。论文还提出了通用的仅YZ平面测量模式，建立了与仅XZ平面模式的联系，并展示了如何在奇偶架构中将寄存器逻辑图嵌入到纯局部相互作用图中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究测量基量子计算（MBQC）中测量平面限制对计算能力的影响，特别是当输入与输出重合时的均匀确定性MBQC情况。旨在理解不同测量平面（YZ平面和XZ平面）模式之间的关系，并探索如何在保持局部相互作用的架构中实现这些模式。

Method: 1. 定义寄存器逻辑图类；2. 证明均匀确定性MBQC中，当输入与输出重合时，计算必须由YZ平面测量驱动；3. 提出通用的仅YZ平面测量模式；4. 建立仅YZ平面与仅XZ平面测量模式之间的联系；5. 在奇偶架构中，展示如何将寄存器逻辑图嵌入到纯局部相互作用图中，并扩展到通用量子计算场景。

Result: 1. 证明了输入与输出重合的均匀确定性MBQC必须在YZ平面测量下进行；2. 提出了通用的仅YZ平面测量模式；3. 建立了仅YZ平面与仅XZ平面测量模式之间的明确联系；4. 展示了寄存器逻辑图可以嵌入到具有纯局部相互作用的图中；5. 将结果扩展到通用量子计算场景。

Conclusion: 该研究完成了对限制在布洛赫球主平面测量的通用模式的研究线。结果表明，在特定条件下，MBQC必须使用YZ平面测量，并且这些模式可以有效地嵌入到具有局部相互作用的架构中，为实际实现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We define the class of register-logic graphs and prove that any uniformly deterministic measurement-based quantum computation (MBQC) where the inputs coincide with the outputs must be driven on such graphs by measurements in the $YZ$ plane of the Bloch sphere. This observation is revisited in the context that goes beyond uniform determinism, where we present a universal $YZ$-plane-only measurement pattern and establish a connection between $YZ$-plane-only and $XZ$-plane-only patterns. These results conclude the line of research on universal patterns with measurements restricted to one of the principal planes of the Bloch sphere. We further demonstrate, within the framework of the Parity Architecture, that $YZ$-plane patterns with the register-logic graph can be embedded into another graph with purely local interactions, and we extend this case to the scenario of universal quantum computation.

</details>


### [110] [PAEMS: Precise and Adaptive Error Model for Superconducting Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29439)
*Songhuan He,Yifei Cui,Cheng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: PAEMS：一种精确自适应的量子比特误差模型，通过比特级分离框架和泄漏传播机制，显著提升了量子纠错解码器的准确性，在多个量子平台上超越了现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器因硬件限制无法生成大规模量子纠错数据集，现有误差模型存在精度不足或计算复杂度指数爆炸的问题，需要开发更精确高效的误差模型来支持量子纠错解码器。

Method: 提出PAEMS模型，采用比特级分离框架，结合泄漏传播机制，捕捉跨时空域的误差演化。通过重复码实验数据集，利用端到端优化流程识别内在量子比特误差。

Result: 在IBM量子处理器上实验显示：时间相关误差降低19.5倍，空间相关误差降低9.3倍，时空相关误差降低5.2倍。在IBM、中国移动、QuantumCTek等多个量子平台上，准确率比Google SI1000模型提升58-73%。

Conclusion: PAEMS模型通过创新的比特级分离和泄漏传播框架，有效解决了现有误差模型的精度和复杂度问题，为量子纠错解码器提供了更精确的合成数据生成方案，在多个量子平台上展现出卓越性能。

Abstract: Superconducting quantum processor units (QPUs) are incapable of producing massive datasets for quantum error correction (QEC) because of hardware limitations. Thus, QEC decoders heavily depend on synthetic data from qubit error models. Classic depolarizing error models with polynomial complexity present limited accuracy. Coherent density matrix methods suffer from exponential complexity $\propto O(4^n)$ where $n$ represents the number of qubits. This paper introduces PAEMS: a precise and adaptive qubit error model. Its qubit-wise separation framework, incorporating leakage propagation, captures error evolvements crossing spatial and temporal domains. Utilizing repetition-code experiment datasets, PAEMS effectively identifies the intrinsic qubit errors through an end-to-end optimization pipeline. Experiments on IBM's QPUs have demonstrated a 19.5$\times$, 9.3$\times$, and 5.2$\times$ reduction in timelike, spacelike, and spacetime error correlation, respectively, surpassing all of the previous works. It also outperforms the accuracy of Google's SI1000 error model by 58$\sim$73\% on multiple quantum platforms, including IBM's Brisbane, Sherbrooke, and Torino, as well as China Mobile's Wuyue and QuantumCTek's Tianyan.

</details>


### [111] [Open Quantum Systems from Dynamical Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29489)
*Yu Su,Yao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于约束量子化的开放量子系统新框架：环境不是预先引入的独立部分，而是从初始约束系统的动力学激活中涌现出来


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统开放量子系统描述需要显式引入系统-环境分解和相互作用哈密顿量，本文旨在提供一种替代框架，从约束量子化角度重新理解环境的起源

Method: 采用狄拉克量子化方法，将物理自由度定义为系统，而约束扇区在获得自身动力学后作为环境；系统-环境耦合不是通过单独的相互作用项添加，而是直接编码在约束结构中

Result: 以量子粒子耦合到布朗振子环境为例，展示了环境影响可以在这种基于约束的设置中表述，验证了框架的有效性

Conclusion: 为量子环境的起源提供了新视角，并指向一个基于约束量子化的开放量子系统通用框架

Abstract: Open quantum systems are traditionally described by decomposing the total Hilbert space into a system and an external environment, linked by an explicit interaction Hamiltonian. We propose an alternative framework in which the environment is not introduced as an independent sector a priori, but instead emerges from the dynamical activation of constraints in an initially constrained quantum system. Within Dirac quantization, the physical degrees of freedom define the system, whereas the constraint sector, once promoted to carry its own dynamics, functions as an environment. In this picture, the system-environment coupling is not added through a separate interaction term, but is encoded directly in the constraint structure. As an example, we study a quantum particle coupled to a Brownian-oscillator environment and show how the resulting environmental influence can be formulated in this constraint-based setting. Our results provide a new perspective on the origin of quantum environments and point toward a general framework for open quantum systems rooted in constrained quantization.

</details>


### [112] [Junction-Intrinsic Dissipation in Hybrid Superconductor-Semiconductor Gatemon Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29498)
*Zhenhai Sun,David Feldstein-Bofill,Ksenia Shagalov,Amalie T. J. Paulsen,Casper Wied,Shikhar Singh,Brian D. Isakov,Jacob Hastrup,Christopher W. Warren,Svend Krøjer,Anders Kringhøj,András Gyenis,Morten Kjaergaard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过共制造gatemon和SIS结transmon量子比特，发现gatemon的弛豫时间比transmon短一个数量级，主要原因是结内禀的温度无关损耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究为什么基于超导-半导体混合约瑟夫森结的gatemon量子比特的弛豫时间远低于最先进的transmon量子比特，并确定损耗机制

Method: 在同一芯片上共制造gatemon和SIS结transmon量子比特，保持电路布局、栅介质和控制线相同，仅约瑟夫森结不同；使用transmon作为片上参考，构建损耗预算；进行温度相关的T1测量

Result: Transmon的弛豫时间达到数十微秒，而gatemon仅几微秒；损耗预算分析表明Purcell衰减、控制线自发辐射和内部介电损耗对应的T1极限比所有测量值高一个数量级；温度相关测量显示两种结具有相似的超导能隙，表明gatemon相干性降低主要来自结内禀的温度无关损耗

Conclusion: gatemon量子比特弛豫时间受限的主要原因是约瑟夫森结内禀的温度无关损耗，而非外部损耗机制，这为改进gatemon设计提供了关键方向

Abstract: Superconducting transmon qubits based on hybrid superconductor-semiconductor Josephson junctions (gatemons) offer gate tunability, but their relaxation times remain well below those of state-of-the-art transmons, and the origin of this discrepancy is not fully understood. Here, we co-fabricate gatemons and SIS-junction transmons with nominally identical circuit layouts, gate dielectrics, and control lines, so that the Josephson element is the only intentional distinction. Across multiple chips, transmons in this architecture reach relaxation times in the tens of microseconds, whereas gatemons saturate in the few-microsecond range. Using the transmons as on-chip references, we construct a loss budget including Purcell decay, spontaneous emission through the control line, and internal dielectric loss, and find that the corresponding T1 limits exceed all measured gatemon values by more than an order of magnitude. Temperature-dependent T1 measurements follow a common quasiparticle-activation model and yield similar superconducting gaps for S-Sm-S and SIS junctions, indicating that the reduced gatemon coherence is dominated by additional temperature-independent, junction-intrinsic dissipation.

</details>


### [113] [Non-perturbative CPMG scaling and qutrit-driven breakdown under compiled superconducting-qubit control: a single-qubit study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29525)
*Jun Ye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过EmuPlat平台将指令集架构级波形生成与层次运动方程(HEOM)结合，在1/f非马尔可夫纯退相干下研究超导transmon三能级系统的CPMG标度行为，揭示了非微扰区域中Y-CPMG的轴依赖标度律破坏现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子比特的退相干源于多能级动力学与结构化环境噪声的相互作用，但微扰理论模型无法捕捉所有相关特征。需要非微扰方法研究在1/f非马尔可夫纯退相干下CPMG标度行为，特别是波形级实现差异对可观测量的影响。

Method: 使用EmuPlat平台，将指令集架构级波形生成与层次运动方程(HEOM)耦合，在1/f非马尔可夫纯退相干环境下模拟三能级超导transmon系统。比较X-CPMG和Y-CPMG脉冲序列的标度行为，分析标准实现与VPPU实现的波形级差异。

Result: Y-CPMG表现出轴依赖标度律破坏：非单调退相干、部分相干恢复、显著的X-Y布居不对称(0.204 vs <0.01)，由三能级非谐性被浴记忆放大驱动。X-CPMG保持良好幂律标度，具有有限n瞬态超额，与非马尔可夫浴记忆效应一致。波形级差异(标准vs VPPU实现)在所有耦合强度下均不可检测，表明旋转框架纯退相干耦合使控制层细节对标度可观测量不可见。

Conclusion: 研究定义了可测试的预测，其中最具实验可及性的仅需定性验证。发现非微扰区域中Y-CPMG的标度律破坏由三能级非谐性和浴记忆效应共同驱动，而旋转框架纯退相干耦合使控制实现细节对退相干标度不可观测，这对量子控制优化具有重要意义。

Abstract: Decoherence in superconducting qubits emerges from the interplay of multilevel dynamics and structured environmental noise, yet perturbative models cannot capture all resulting signatures. Here, EmuPlat couples instruction-set-architecture-level waveform generation to the hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) under $1/f$ non-Markovian pure dephasing. In the resulting non-perturbative regime -- where filter-function predictions become quantitatively uninformative -- CPMG scaling of a three-level superconducting transmon yields one calibration result, two physical findings, and one structural null. Y-CPMG exhibits axis-dependent scaling-law breakdown -- non-monotonic decoherence, partial coherence revival, and pronounced X--Y population asymmetry ($0.204$ vs ${<}\,0.01$) -- driven by third-level anharmonicity amplified by bath memory; X-CPMG maintains well-behaved power-law scaling with a finite-$n$ transient excess consistent with non-Markovian bath-memory effects. The structural null is equally informative: waveform-level differences -- Standard versus VPPU realizations -- remain undetectable across all coupling strengths, establishing that rotating-frame pure-dephasing coupling renders control-layer detail invisible to scaling observables. These findings define testable predictions, the most experimentally accessible requiring only qualitative verification.

</details>


### [114] [Logical-to-Physical Compilation for Reducing Depth in Distributed Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29536)
*Folkert de Ronde,Stephan Wong,Sebastian Feld*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个编译器，通过逻辑到物理分解与深度感知重调度相结合，减少分布式量子电路的执行成本，降低电路深度，提高NISQ时代量子算法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算通过连接多个小型处理器构建更强大的系统，但会引入显著的电路深度开销。逻辑操作分解为需要纠缠生成的顺序物理过程，这些顺序操作在NISQ时代因噪声而限制量子算法的可靠性。

Method: 开发一个编译器，集成逻辑到物理分解与深度感知重调度。识别共享控制或目标量子位的逻辑CNOT门序列，将其重调度为并行指令组，并应用允许使用分布式共享纠缠资源同时执行多个门的分解方法。提出确保在可能时创建并行性、保持逻辑等价且不增加电路深度的算法。

Result: 基准测试结果表明，编译器能持续减少包含固有顺序CNOT结构的电路深度，而对已经并行的电路保持不变。这些结果突显了调度与硬件感知分解相结合的价值。

Conclusion: 该编译器是提高分布式量子计算保真度的实用工具，通过结合调度和硬件感知分解，有效减少分布式量子电路的执行成本，为NISQ时代的量子算法提供更可靠的实现方案。

Abstract: Quantum computing is expected to become a foundational technology for solving problems that exceed the capabilities of classical systems. As quantum algorithms and hardware technologies continue to advance, the need for scalable architectures becomes increasingly clear. Distributed quantum computing offers a promising path forward by interconnecting multiple smaller processors into a larger, more powerful system. However, distributed quantum computing introduces significant circuit depth overhead, as logical operations are typically decomposed into sequential physical procedures that require entanglement generation. These sequential operations limit the reliability of quantum algorithms in the NISQ era due to noise. In this work, we present a compiler that integrates logical-to-physical decomposition with depth-aware rescheduling to reduce the execution cost of distributed quantum circuits. The compiler identifies sequences of logical CNOT gates that share a control or target qubit, reschedules them into parallel instruction groups, and applies decompositions that allow multiple gates to be executed simultaneously using distributed shared entanglement resources. An algorithm is proposed that ensures parallelism is created when possible while keeping logical equivalence and that circuit depth is never increased. Benchmark results demonstrate that the compiler consistently reduces circuit depth for circuits containing inherently sequential CNOT structures, while leaving already-parallel circuits unchanged. These results highlight the value of combining scheduling and hardware-aware decomposition, and establish the compiler as a practical tool for improving the fidelity of distributed quantum computations.

</details>


### [115] [Reducing Complexity for Quantum Approaches in Train Load Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29543)
*Zhijie Tang,Albert Nieto-Morales,Arit Kumar Bishwas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧凑的列车装载优化模型，通过隐式计算重新处理成本，避免了传统方法中需要大量二元变量和约束的问题，显著减小了模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统列车装载优化模型需要为每个可能的重新处理操作引入显式二元变量和复杂的逻辑约束，导致模型规模庞大、求解困难。需要一种更紧凑高效的建模方法来解决这一组合优化问题。

Method: 提出了一种创新的紧凑数学规划模型，将重新处理成本隐式地计算在目标函数中，避免了专门的重新处理变量和相关约束。通过形式化比较分析变量和约束数量的减少，并采用模拟退火元启发式算法评估模型效果。

Result: 新模型显著减少了变量和约束的数量，通过模拟退火算法在各种问题实例中找到了高质量的装载方案，证明了模型不仅更简洁，而且在实际应用中有效。

Conclusion: 该紧凑模型为列车装载优化问题提供了可扩展且强大的解决方案，是现代铁路物流的有效工具，通过避免显式重新处理变量实现了模型规模的显著减小。

Abstract: Efficiently planning container loads onto trains is a computationally challenging combinatorial optimization problem, central to logistics and supply chain management. A primary source of this complexity arises from the need to model and reduce rehandle operations-unproductive crane moves required to access blocked containers. Conventional mathematical formulations address this by introducing explicit binary variables and a web of logical constraints for each potential rehandle, resulting in large-scale models that are difficult to solve. This paper presents a fundamental departure from this paradigm. We introduce an innovative and compact mathematical formulation for the Train Load Optimization (TLO) problem where the rehandle cost is calculated implicitly within the objective function. This novel approach helps prevent the need for dedicated rehandle variables and their associated constraints, leading to a dramatic reduction in model size. We provide a formal comparison against a conventional model to analytically demonstrate the significant reduction in the number of variables and constraints. The efficacy of our compact formulation is assessed through a simulated annealing metaheuristic, which finds high-quality loading plans for various problem instances. The results confirm that our model is not only more parsimonious but also practically effective, offering a scalable and powerful tool for modern rail logistics.

</details>


### [116] [Adiabatic Ramsey Interferometry for Measuring Weak Nonlinearities with Super-Heisenberg Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29574)
*Venelin P. Pavlov,Bogomila S. Nikolova,Peter A. Ivanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于绝热Ramsey干涉的囚禁离子弱非线性检测技术，利用量子Rabi模型作为探针，通过测量自旋态概率高效估计非线性耦合，精度可达超海森堡极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要检测囚禁离子系统中的弱非线性效应，这些非线性可能来自势阱的非谐项或库仑相互作用的高阶项。传统方法需要特定纠缠态制备，限制了实际应用。

Method: 采用绝热Ramsey干涉技术，以量子Rabi模型作为探针系统。通过测量自旋态概率来估计非线性耦合参数，无需特定纠缠态制备，甚至可以从初始热运动态开始。

Result: 自旋信号被平均声子激发放大，使得参数估计精度达到超海森堡极限。即使在弱自旋退相干存在的情况下，也能观察到超海森堡标度。

Conclusion: 该方法为检测囚禁离子系统中的弱非线性提供了一种高效方案，无需复杂纠缠态制备，对初始态要求宽松，且对退相干具有一定鲁棒性，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose an adiabatic Ramsey interferometry technique for detecting weak nonlinearities with trapped ions. The method relies on using the quantum Rabi model as a probe, which is sensitive to nonlinear symmetry-breaking perturbations. We show that the couplings which arise either from anharmonic terms of the trapping potential or due to higher order terms in the Coulomb interaction expansion can be efficiently estimated by measuring the spin state probabilities alone. We show that the spin signal is amplified by the mean-phonon excitations, which results in the estimation precision reaching the super-Heisenberg limit. Notably, achieving such high-precision estimation does not require specific entangled state preparation and can be reached even for initial thermal motion state. Furthermore, we show that the super-Heisenberg scaling can be observed even in the presence of weak spin-dephasing.

</details>


### [117] [Entanglement in prepare-and-measure scenarios without receiver inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29625)
*Elna Svegborn,Armin Tavakoli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了无独立测量输入的prepare-and-measure场景，揭示了量子优势的基本特征，包括经典消息和量子消息两种情况下的量子优势机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无独立测量输入的prepare-and-measure场景在概念上自然，且具有实际意义，可作为黑盒认证自适应单向LOCC的测试平台。

Method: 系统研究无独立测量输入的prepare-and-measure场景：1) 对于经典消息，识别最小和次最小量子优势场景；2) 对于量子消息，要求接收者在测量纠缠粒子前读取消息，分析非投影测量在量子优势中的作用。

Result: 1) 经典消息的最小量子优势场景需要高维纠缠才能最大化；2) 次最小场景的量子优势可由CHSH型非定域性驱动，适合认证实验；3) 量子消息结合非投影读取能放大量子优势，挑战了非投影测量在黑盒实验中次要作用的传统观点。

Conclusion: 无独立测量输入的prepare-and-measure场景揭示了量子优势的基本特征，非投影测量在黑盒实验中扮演不可或缺的角色，挑战了传统认知，为量子认证提供了新视角。

Abstract: The most elementary prepare-and-measure scenarios have no independent measurement inputs. No inputs mean that quantum advantages require two indispensable ingredients: shared entanglement and measurements that can be adapted to the communicated messages. Understanding these scenarios is therefore conceptually natural, but also practically relevant, since they act as testbeds for black-box certification of adaptive one-way LOCC. Here, we study them systematically and reveal several of their basic features. For classical messages, we first identify the minimal scenario with a quantum advantage and show that it is maximised by high-dimensional entanglement. Then, we identify the next-to-minimal scenario, and show that quantum advantages can be propelled by nonlocality of the Clauser-Horne-Shimony-Holt type, which makes this an appropriate setting for certification experiments. Proceeding further, we replace classical messages with quantum messages, but require the receiver to read the message before measuring the entangled particle. We show that this leads to amplified quantum advantages, that are made possible only thanks to non-projective message read-out. This in dispensable role of non-projective measurements challenges the common wisdom that they play a secondary role in revealing the power of quantum correlations in black-box experiments.

</details>


### [118] [Non-Equilibrium Sock Dynamics: Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking in the Agitated Wash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29650)
*Ahmad Darwish,Matteo Murdaca,Jami J. Kinnunen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种袜子动力学的准粒子理论，将袜子建模为受搅动洗衣凝聚物的玻色子激发，解释了袜子不成对、缩水等现象的物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决日常生活中普遍观察到的袜子不成对现象，从物理学角度为这一常见现象提供理论解释，将日常经验与量子物理概念联系起来。

Method: 采用准粒子理论框架，将袜子建模为玻色子激发，分析袜子在不同材料中的色散关系，研究Beliaev衰变、Landau-Khalatnikov散射以及旋转滚筒通过动态卡西米尔效应产生袜子-反袜子对等过程。

Result: 建立了袜子动力学的完整理论模型，解释了袜子缩水、分裂成两双低动量袜子、降解为棉绒和松散线头等现象，并揭示了洗涤结束时不成对袜子的根本模糊性：既可能是其配对袜子的破坏，也可能是全新袜子的自发产生。

Conclusion: 袜子不成对现象可以通过准粒子理论得到系统解释，该理论将日常观察与凝聚态物理概念相结合，为理解复杂系统中的随机过程提供了新颖的视角。

Abstract: It is a universal empirical observation that socks become unpaired in the laundry. We propose a quasiparticle theory of sock dynamics in which individual socks are modelled as bosonic excitations of the agitated laundry condensate. The sock dispersion relation is material-dependent: nondispersive materials retain their shape, while dispersive materials give rise to the well-documented phenomenon of sock shrinkage. In the convex regions of the dispersive spectrum, socks undergo Beliaev decay and spontaneously split into two lower-momentum socks, while in the concave regions the dominant process is Landau-Khalatnikov scattering, which degrades socks into lint and loose threads. In addition, the rotating drum creates sock-antisock pairs from the laundry vacuum via the dynamical Casimir effect. The coexistence of these creation and destruction channels gives rise to a fundamental ambiguity: an unpaired sock at the end of a wash cycle is equally consistent with the destruction of its partner or the spontaneous creation of an entirely new sock.

</details>


### [119] [The Manipulate-and-Observe Attack on Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29669)
*William Tighe,George Brumpton,Mark Carney,Benjamin T. H. Varcoe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对量子密钥分发后处理阶段的新型攻击——操纵与观察攻击，该攻击结合量子传输阶段的拦截和错误注入，以及后处理阶段的奇偶校验信息泄露利用，能够显著降低安全性甚至完全恢复密钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子密钥分发虽然基于量子力学原理提供了无条件安全的密钥交换，但经典后处理阶段存在漏洞。现有研究缺乏对奇偶校验信息泄露在协调过程中可能被主动操纵的全面威胁模型。该论文旨在填补这一安全空白，揭示量子密钥分发系统中量子传输和经典后处理结合的安全风险。

Method: 提出操纵与观察攻击，包含两个阶段：1）量子传输阶段：攻击者拦截部分量子比特（比例ρ），注入最大可容忍错误（达到11%错误阈值）而不被检测；2）后处理阶段：攻击者探测协调过程中的最大奇偶校验信息泄露，利用向量化并行暴力过滤算法将搜索空间从2^n缩小到极少候选密钥。通过模拟实验，将该攻击应用于最广泛使用的BB84协议和基准协调协议Cascade。

Result: 模拟结果显示，该攻击能够显著降低安全性至理论界限以下，在最坏情况下能够完全恢复协调后的密钥材料。攻击原理可能威胁其他基于奇偶校验的协调方案，如低密度奇偶校验（LDPC），突显了需要紧急考虑密钥交换和后处理组合安全性的重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了量子密钥分发系统中量子传输和经典后处理结合的安全漏洞，提出了首个针对奇偶校验信息泄露被主动操纵的全面威胁模型。操纵与观察攻击能够显著破坏量子密钥分发的安全性，强调了需要重新评估和加强后处理协议的安全性，特别是针对基于奇偶校验的协调方案。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution is often regarded as an unconditionally secure method to exchange a secret key by harnessing fundamental aspects of quantum mechanics. Despite the robustness of key exchange, classical post-processing reveals vulnerabilities that an eavesdropper could target. In particular, many reconciliation protocols correct errors by comparing the parities of subsets between both parties. These communications occur over insecure channels, leaking information that an eavesdropper could exploit. Currently there is no holistic threat model that addresses how parity-leakage during reconciliation might be actively manipulated. In this paper we introduce a new form of attack, namely the Manipulate-and-Observe attack in which the adversary (1) partially intercepts a fraction $ρ$ of the qubits during key exchange, injecting the maximally tolerated amount of errors up to the 11 percent error threshold whilst remaining undetected and (2) probes the maximum amount of parity-leakage during reconciliation, and exploits it using a vectorised, parallel brute force filter to shrink the search space from 2n down to as few as a single candidate, for an n-bit reconciled key. We perform simulations of the attack, deploying it on the most widely used protocol, BB84, andthe benchmark reconciliation protocol, Cascade. Our simulation results demonstrate that the attack can significantly reduce the security below the theoretical bound and, in the worst case, fully recover the reconciled key material. The principles of the attack could threaten other parity-based reconciliation schemes, like Low Density Parity Check, which underscores the need for urgent consideration of the combined security of key exchange and post-processing.

</details>


### [120] [Probes of chaos over the Clifford group and approach to Haar values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29695)
*Stefano Cusumano,Gianluca Esposito,Alioscia Hamma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 使用等谱旋转技术分析量子混沌探针从稳定子基到随机基的转变，并研究其在随机矩阵理论、高斯对角系综、高斯幺正系综和Toric Code哈密顿量上的平均行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统的混沌行为可以通过给定探针的期望值对Haar分布矩的遵循程度来表征。本研究旨在使用等谱旋转技术分析多种混沌探针的行为，特别关注从稳定子基到随机基的转变过程。

Method: 采用等谱旋转技术，固定哈密顿量谱并随机选取其特征向量。通过T掺杂随机量子电路研究从稳定子基到Haar测度随机基的转变。计算探针在随机矩阵理论、高斯对角系综（非混沌）和高斯幺正系综（混沌）随机谱系综上的平均值，并分析在Toric Code哈密顿量上的行为。

Result: 论文分析了多种混沌探针在不同系综和哈密顿量上的行为，揭示了从稳定子基到随机基的转变特征，以及探针在混沌和非混沌系统中的表现差异。

Conclusion: 等谱旋转技术为分析量子混沌探针提供了有效框架，能够清晰表征从稳定子基到随机基的转变过程，并区分混沌和非混沌系统的行为特征，为量子混沌研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Chaotic behavior of quantum systems can be characterized by the adherence of the expectation values of given probes to moments of the Haar distribution. In this work, we analyze the behavior of several probes of chaos using a technique known as Isospectral Twirling [1]. This consists in fixing the spectrum of the Hamiltonian and picking its eigenvectors at random. Here, we study the transition from stabilizer bases to random bases according to the Haar measure by T-doped random quantum circuits. We then compute the average value of the probes over ensembles of random spectra from Random Matrix Theory, the Gaussian Diagonal Ensemble and the Gaussian Unitary Ensemble, associated with non-chaotic and chaotic behavior respectively. We also study the behavior of such probes over the Toric Code Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [121] [Optimal Control of Spin Squeezing in 2D Finite-Range Interacting Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29737)
*Ang Li,Ling-Na Wu,Li You*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种最优控制策略，用于在具有偶极相互作用的二维XX模型中实现增强的自旋压缩，通过优化单个集体横向场显著超越双轴扭曲基准，并在开放边界条件和退相干噪声下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋压缩既是量子纠缠的基本见证，也是量子增强计量学的关键资源。在有限范围相互作用系统中产生显著的自旋压缩仍然具有挑战性，但这种能力对于推进量子技术至关重要。

Method: 开发了最优控制策略，利用转子-自旋波理论处理周期性边界条件，避免了计算瓶颈，探索了前所未有的规模控制策略。优化单个集体横向场，动态抑制有限范围相互作用引起的子空间混合，将系统演化主要限制在最大自旋子空间内。

Result: 优化控制场实现了显著的自旋压缩增强，超越了双轴扭曲基准。该策略在开放边界条件和退相干噪声下仍然有效，展示了其鲁棒性和实验实施的适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种可扩展的框架，用于在现实系统中实现增强的自旋压缩，最优控制策略通过动态抑制子空间混合有效提升了自旋压缩性能，为量子技术应用提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Spin squeezing serves as both a fundamental witness of quantum entanglement and a critical resource for quantum-enhanced metrology. While generating substantial spin squeezing in finite-range interacting systems remains challenging, such capability is important for advancing quantum technologies. In this work, we develop an optimal control strategy for achieving enhanced spin squeezing in a two-dimensional XX model with dipolar interactions. Leveraging rotor-spin-wave theory for periodic boundary conditions, we circumvent computational bottlenecks to explore control strategies at unprecedented scales. Remarkably, optimizing a single collective transverse field is sufficient to achieve substantial squeezing enhancement, exceeding the two-axis-twisting benchmark. The optimized control field achieves this breakthrough by dynamically suppressing inter-subspace mixing induced by the finite-range interactions, thereby confining the system evolution predominantly within the maximal spin subspace. We further extend rotor-spin-wave theory to open boundary conditions and incorporate dephasing noise, providing a scalable framework for realistic systems. Under these conditions, the optimized protocol remains effective, highlighting its robustness and suitability for experimental implementation.

</details>


### [122] [Nonequilibrium energy transport in driven-dissipative quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29754)
*Junran Kong,Yuwei Lu,Huan Liu,Liwei Duan,Chen Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种在驱动耗散量子系统中研究非平衡能量输运的驱动量子主方程方法，验证了其有效性并展示了驱动相位对能量交换过程的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡能量输运是量子热力学和量子技术中的基本问题，需要一种有效方法来研究同时受热力学偏压和相干驱动场作用的驱动耗散量子系统中的能量流动。

Method: 提出了在"着装"图像下的驱动量子主方程方法，通过与Floquet主方程比较验证其有效性，并在一般自旋和玻色子模型中分析能量流。

Result: 驱动量子主方程具有普适适用性；系统-库相互作用中保留的附加驱动相位会显著改变微观能量交换过程；稳态能量流在共振区域附近显著增强；传统着装主方程给出不同的能量流行为。

Conclusion: 驱动量子主方程为控制驱动耗散纳米器件中的量子输运和热力学性能提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium energy transport serves as one of fundamental problems in quantum thermodynamics and quantum technologies. Driven quantum master equation in the dressed picture provides an efficient way of investigating nonequilibrium energy flow in general driven-dissipative quantum systems, where the systems are simultaneously driven by the finite thermodynamic bias and coherent driving field. The validity and general applicability of driven quantum master equation is confirmed by comparing with Floquet master equation, by analyzing energy currents in generic spin and boson models. The additional driving phase reserved in system-reservoir interactions, will apparently modify microscopic energy exchange processes. The steady-state energy currents are dramatically enhanced, in particular near the resonant regimes. In contrast, the traditional dressed master equation yields distinct behaviors of the energy currents. We hope that the driven quantum master equation may provide an efficient utility for the control of quantum transport and thermodynamic performances in driven-dissipative nanodevices.

</details>


### [123] [Topological sum rule for geometric phases of quantum gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29795)
*Nadav Orion,Boris Rotstein,Nirron Miller,Eric Akkermans*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文建立了一个拓扑求和规则，将两量子比特系统在完整初始态基上积累的几何相与哈密顿量的绕数联系起来，揭示了拓扑分类与纠缠生成之间的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子系统的拓扑性质与量子信息处理能力之间的关系，特别是探索哈密顿量的拓扑分类如何影响量子门的实现和纠缠生成。

Method: 建立拓扑求和规则 ν_U = (1/2π)∑_n γ_n = 2mν_H，连接两量子比特系统的几何相累积与哈密顿量绕数。通过Wootters并发度测量不同拓扑类实现的量子门之间的相分布差异。

Result: 证明不同拓扑类实现的相同量子门必须以不同方式分布几何相，这种差异可通过Wootters并发度测量。发现非平凡拓扑是生成纠缠的必要条件：只有ν_H ≠ 0的哈密顿量才能产生纠缠。

Conclusion: 拓扑性质与量子信息处理能力存在深刻联系，非平凡拓扑是纠缠生成的必要条件，这为量子计算中的拓扑资源利用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We establish a topological sum rule, $ν_U = \frac{1}{2π}\sum_nγ_n = 2mν_H$, connecting the geometric phases accumulated by a two-qubit system over a complete basis of initial states to the winding number $ν_H$ classifying its Hamiltonian. Implementations of the same gate from different topological classes must distribute these phases differently, making their distinction measurable through the Wootters concurrence. As a corollary, nontrivial topology is a necessary condition for entanglement: only Hamiltonians with access to $ν_H \neq 0$ can generate it.

</details>


### [124] [Certifying and learning local quantum Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29809)
*Andreas Bluhm,Matthias C. Caro,Francisco Escudero Gutiérrez,Junseo Lee,Aadil Oufkir,Cambyse Rouzé,Myeongjin Shin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了量子k-局部哈密顿量的认证与学习问题，提出了三个主要贡献：哈密顿量认证的优化算法、吉布斯态的高效学习算法、以及吉布斯态的高效认证算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子k-局部哈密顿量的认证和学习问题，旨在解决现有方法在样本复杂性和时间效率方面的局限性，特别是针对哈密顿量性质测试的最优算法和吉布斯态的高效学习与认证。

Method: 1. 哈密顿量认证：通过时间演化算子访问，在归一化Frobenius范数下实现哈密顿量认证，利用Bonami超收缩引理进行分析；2. 吉布斯态学习：设计在迹范数下学习局部哈密顿量吉布斯态的算法；3. 吉布斯态认证：开发在迹范数下认证吉布斯态的算法。

Result: 1. 哈密顿量认证达到O(1/ε)演化时间，与Heisenberg尺度下界Ω(1/ε)匹配，是首个测试哈密顿量性质的最优算法；2. 吉布斯态学习算法在所有相关参数上具有样本效率；3. 吉布斯态认证算法在样本和时间上均高效，解决了Anshu提出的问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子k-局部哈密顿量的认证和学习提供了高效算法，在哈密顿量性质测试方面实现了最优性，在吉布斯态学习和认证方面显著改进了现有方法的效率，推动了量子信息处理中哈密顿量分析的发展。

Abstract: In this work, we study the problems of certifying and learning quantum $k$-local Hamiltonians, for a constant $k$. Our main contributions are as follows:
  - Certification of Hamiltonians. We show that certifying a local Hamiltonian in normalized Frobenius norm via access to its time-evolution operator can be achieved with only $O(1/\varepsilon)$ evolution time. This is optimal, as it matches the Heisenberg-scaling lower bound of $Ω(1/\varepsilon)$. To our knowledge, this is the first optimal algorithm for testing a Hamiltonian property. A key ingredient in our analysis is the Bonami Hypercontractivity Lemma from Fourier analysis.
  - Learning Gibbs states. We design an algorithm for learning Gibbs states of local Hamiltonians in trace norm that is sample-efficient in all relevant parameters. In contrast, previous approaches learned the underlying Hamiltonian (which implies learning the Gibbs state), and thus inevitably suffered from exponential sample complexity scaling in the inverse temperature.
  - Certification of Gibbs states. We give an algorithm for certifying Gibbs states of local Hamiltonians in trace norm that is both sample and time-efficient in all relevant parameters, thereby solving a question posed by Anshu (Harvard Data Science Review, 2022).

</details>


### [125] [Floquet Codes from Derived Semi-Regular Hyperbolic Tessellations on Orientable and Non-Orientable Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29811)
*Douglas F. Copatti,Giuliano G. La Guardia,Waldir S. Soares,Edson D. Carvalho,Eduardo B. Silva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了在可定向和不可定向紧致曲面上的新型量子Floquet码，通过将曲面与双曲多边形关联并研究双曲半规则镶嵌来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展双曲Floquet码的构造方法，使其适用于更广泛的曲面类型，包括可定向和不可定向紧致曲面，以丰富量子纠错码的几何构造框架。

Method: 将曲面与双曲多边形关联，研究曲面上的双曲半规则镶嵌，并推广先前关于亏格g≥2的连通紧致曲面上的双曲Floquet码构造方法。

Result: 成功构造了多个在可定向和不可定向紧致曲面上的新型量子Floquet码，并进行了性能分析和渐近行为研究。

Conclusion: 提出的构造方法有效扩展了双曲Floquet码的应用范围，为在更广泛几何结构上构建量子纠错码提供了系统框架，并展示了良好的渐近性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct several new quantum Floquet codes on compact, orientable, as well as non-orientable surfaces. In order to obtain such codes, we identify these surfaces with hyperbolic polygons and examine hyperbolic semi-regular tessellations on such surfaces. The method of construction presented here generalizes similar constructions concerning hyperbolic Floquet codes on connected and compact surfaces with genus $g \geq 2$. A performance analysis and an investigation of the asymptotic behavior of these codes are also presented.

</details>


### [126] [Trotter Scars: Trotter Error Suppression in Quantum Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29857)
*Bozhen Zhou,Qi Zhao,Pan Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Trotter误差的初始态依赖性，提出了"Trotter疤痕"概念，并开发了寻找误差最小化初始态的变分框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明Trotter误差具有高度初始态依赖性，标准上界常常显著高估误差。然而，异常小Trotter误差的机制以及系统识别误差弹性态的方法仍不清楚

Method: 使用相互作用图像微扰理论推导哈密顿量本征基中领头阶Trotter误差的解析表达式；引入通用变分框架寻找给定哈密顿量的误差最小化初始态；在多个自旋模型中应用该框架

Result: 分析表明，在谱可公度能量梯子上支持的初始态表现出强烈抑制的误差增长和持续的Loschmidt复兴；优化态的光谱支持和动力学行为与微扰预测一致

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了Trotter误差弹性的光谱起源，并为数字量子模拟中误差弹性态的发现提供了实用策略

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that Trotter errors are highly initial-state dependent and that standard upper bounds often substantially overestimate them. However, the mechanism underlying anomalously small Trotter errors and a systematic route to identifying error-resilient states remain unclear. Using interaction-picture perturbation theory, we derive an analytical expression for the leading-order Trotter error in the eigenbasis of the Hamiltonian. Our analysis shows that initial states supported on spectrally commensurate energy ladders exhibit strongly suppressed error growth together with persistent Loschmidt revivals. We refer to such states as Trotter scars. To identify such states in practice, we further introduce a general variational framework for finding error-minimizing initial states for a given Hamiltonian. Applying this framework to several spin models, we find optimized states whose spectral support and dynamical behavior agree with the perturbative prediction. Our results reveal the spectral origin of Trotter-error resilience and provide a practical strategy for discovering error-resilient states in digital quantum simulation.

</details>


### [127] [Weak-Field Expansion: A Time-Closed Solution of Quantum Three-Wave Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29869)
*Hanzhong Zhang,Avi Pe'er*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种超越经典无损耗驱动场标准近似的三线性玻色哈密顿量系统海森堡演化的系统推导方法，使用输入场振幅的微扰展开而非传统的时间展开，获得时间闭合表达式，在参数增益高时收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在处理强驱动下的三线性玻色哈密顿量系统时，通常采用经典无损耗驱动场的近似，这限制了量子特性的准确描述。需要发展一种能够超越这一标准近似、更精确处理量子驱动场效应的方法。

Method: 采用输入场振幅的微扰展开而非传统的Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff时间展开，对哈密顿量传播子进行系统推导。该方法自动产生时间闭合表达式，在参数增益高时收敛更快，因为其使用的小参数更符合物理问题的本质。

Result: 一阶展开得到光学参量放大和下转换的已知量子解；二阶修正可用于检测时间-能量纠缠；三阶修正可计算强驱动场量子特性对光学模式间量子态传输保真度的限制。该方法比传统BCH展开收敛更快，特别在高参数增益区域。

Conclusion: 提出的基于输入场振幅的微扰展开方法为处理强驱动下的三线性玻色系统提供了更精确、更高效的框架，超越了经典无损耗驱动场的标准近似，能够系统推导高阶修正，在量子信息处理和量子光学应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: We present a systematic derivation of the Heisenberg evolution of a trilinear bosonic Hamiltonian system in presence of a strong drive beyond the standard approximation of a classical, undepleted driving field. We employ a perturbative expansion of the Hamiltonian propagator in orders of the input field amplitudes, as opposed to the standard Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff (BCH) expansion of the propagator in orders of time. Our method automatically provides time-closed expressions; and converges considerably faster than BCH, especially in the regime of high parametric gain because the small parameter it uses is natural to the problem. We obtain the well-known quantum solution for optical parametric amplification of down-conversion simply as the first order of the expansion, and present the rigorous procedure to derive higher order corrections one by one. To demonstrate the utility of higher corrections, we discuss the 2nd order correction to the pump field as an ideal detector of time-energy entanglement in parametric down-conversion. We also use the 3rd order correction to calculate the limits on the fidelity of quantum state-transfer from one optical mode to another using sum/difference frequency generation, due to the quantum properties of the strong driving field.

</details>


### [128] [LLM-Guided Evolutionary Search for Algebraic T-Count Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29894)
*Daniil Fisher,Valentin Khrulkov,Mikhail Saygin,Ivan Oseledets,Stanislav Straupe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: VarTODD：一种基于策略参数化的T门计数优化框架，通过分离代数重写系统与搜索策略，结合LLM引导的进化算法实现量子电路中T门数量的进一步减少


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在容错量子计算中，T门（非克利福德门）的成本远高于克利福德操作，因此减少量子电路中的T门数量是量子编译的核心挑战。现有的T门计数最小化方法（如FastTODD）面临组合优化困难，需要更灵活的优化框架

Method: 提出VarTODD框架，将FastTODD的策略参数化：保持代数变换的正确性不变，将候选生成、池化和动作选择暴露为可调启发式组件。使用GigaEvo（LLM引导的进化框架）自动优化VarTODD策略，分离代数重写质量与搜索策略质量

Result: 在标准算术基准测试中，固定手工设计的VarTODD策略已匹配或超越FastTODD基线：GF(2^9)从147降至139，GF(2^10)从173降至163。通过GigaEvo自动优化策略，在更困难实例上获得额外增益：GF(2^10)达到157，GF(2^16)达到385

Conclusion: 策略优化是改进代数T门计数减少的独立实用杠杆，LLM引导的进化算法为利用这一杠杆提供了可行途径。VarTODD框架通过分离代数变换与搜索策略，为量子编译中的T门优化开辟了新方向

Abstract: Reducing the non-Clifford cost of fault-tolerant quantum circuits is a central challenge in quantum compilation, since T gates are typically far more expensive than Clifford operations in error-corrected architectures. For Clifford+T circuits, minimizing T-count remains a difficult combinatorial problem even for highly structured algebraic optimizers. We introduce VarTODD, a policy-parameterized variant of FastTODD in which the correctness-preserving algebraic transformations are left unchanged while candidate generation, pooling, and action selection are exposed as tunable heuristic components. This separates the quality of the algebraic rewrite system from the quality of the search policy. On standard arithmetic benchmarks, fixed hand-designed VarTODD policies already match or improve strong FastTODD baselines, including reductions from 147 to 139 for GF(2^9) and from 173 to 163 for GF(2^10) in the corresponding benchmark branches. As a proof of principle for automated tuning, we then optimize VarTODD policies with GigaEvo, an LLM-guided evolutionary framework, and obtain additional gains on harder instances, reaching 157 for GF(2^10) and 385 for GF(2^16). These results identify policy optimization as an independent and practical lever for improving algebraic T-count reduction, while LLM-guided evolution provides one viable way to exploit it.

</details>


### [129] [Four Generations of Quantum Biomedical Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29944)
*Xin Jin,Priyam Srivastava,Ronghe Wang,Yuqing Li,Jonathan Beaumariage,Tom Purdy,M. V. Gurudev Dutt,Kang Kim,Kaushik Seshadreesan,Junyu Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于量子资源利用的四代量子生物传感器框架，从经典噪声限制到量子增强智能的演进路线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子传感技术在超灵敏生物医学传感方面具有变革潜力，但其临床转化仍受限于经典噪声极限和对宏观系综的依赖，需要系统性框架来组织不断发展的量子生物传感器领域

Method: 提出基于量子资源利用的统一代际框架：第一代利用离散能级但遵循经典标度律；第二代利用量子相干性达到标准量子极限；第三代利用纠缠和自旋压缩接近海森堡极限；第四代将量子传感与量子学习、变分电路端到端集成

Result: 通过分析带宽匹配和传感器-组织接近度等关键参数，识别了关键技术瓶颈，提出了从测量物理可观测量到提取结构化生物信息的量子增强智能转型路线图

Conclusion: 该代际框架为量子生物传感器的系统分类和发展提供了统一视角，指出了从物理测量到生物信息提取的量子增强智能转型路径，有助于推动量子传感技术的临床转化

Abstract: Quantum sensing technologies offer transformative potential for ultra-sensitive biomedical sensing, yet their clinical translation remains constrained by classical noise limits and a reliance on macroscopic ensembles. We propose a unifying generational framework to organize the evolving landscape of quantum biosensors based on their utilization of quantum resources. First-generation devices utilize discrete energy levels for signal transduction but follow classical scaling laws. Second-generation sensors exploit quantum coherence to reach the standard quantum limit, while third-generation architectures leverage entanglement and spin squeezing to approach Heisenberg-limited precision. We further define an emerging fourth generation characterized by the end-to-end integration of quantum sensing with quantum learning and variational circuits, enabling adaptive inference directly within the quantum domain. By analyzing critical parameters such as bandwidth matching and sensor-tissue proximity, we identify key technological bottlenecks and propose a roadmap for transitioning from measuring physical observables to extracting structured biological information with quantum-enhanced intelligence.

</details>


### [130] [Strong converse bounds on the classical identification capacity of the qubit depolarizing channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29987)
*Liuhang Ye,Bjarne Bergh,Nilanjana Datta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文推导了量子比特去极化通道的强逆界，解决了先前逆界在完全噪声通道下不为零的问题，并在完全乘积测量约束下建立了识别容量等于经典容量的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于量子通道经典识别容量的逆界存在缺陷：即使在完全噪声通道下，Atif等人提出的逆界仍保持正值，这与识别不可能的实际情形不符。需要建立能在完全噪声极限下正确消失的逆界。

Method: 针对量子比特去极化通道（噪声参数p），推导了强逆界（从而也包含弱逆界）。在完全乘积测量约束下的同时经典识别场景中，建立了与可达界匹配的逆界。

Result: 获得了在p→1时消失的强逆界，纠正了完全噪声极限下的错误行为。在完全乘积测量约束下，逆界与可达界匹配，证明识别容量等于通道的经典容量。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了量子通道识别容量逆界的关键问题，建立了在完全噪声极限下行为正确的强逆界，并在特定测量约束下确定了识别容量等于经典容量。

Abstract: A strong converse bound for the classical identification capacity of a quantum channel is an upper bound on the asymptotic identification rate of classical messages sent through the channel, such that, above this rate, the probability of an identification error necessarily converges to one. Converse bounds for identification are notoriously difficult to obtain for fully quantum channels. The only previously known converse bound, due to Atif, Pradhan and Winter [Int.~J.~Quantum Inf.~22(5):2440013, 2024], has the unsatisfactory feature of remaining strictly positive even for a completely noisy channel, for which identification is clearly impossible. We derive strong (and hence also weak) converse bounds, for the qubit depolarizing channel with noise parameter $p$, that vanish as $p\to 1$, thereby yielding the correct behavior in the completely noisy limit. Moreover, in the setting of simultaneous classical identification under the constraint of complete product measurements, our converse bound matches the corresponding achievability bound, and establishes that in this case the identification capacity equals the classical capacity of the channel.

</details>


### [131] [Noise Inference by Recycling Test Rounds in Verification Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30015)
*Amit Saha,Harold Ollivier*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过回收测试轮次数据来持续监控噪声模型参数，从而减轻量子通信验证协议的重复开销，从服务提供商角度优化量子计算验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算验证协议存在两种变体：无量子通信的协议需要服务器端大量开销来实现量子抗性密码原语；有量子通信的协议虽然服务提供商只需重复开销，但在当前量子比特有限的机器上，只有量子通信协议实现了概念验证。需要进一步减轻量子通信协议的重复开销。

Method: 从服务提供商的角度重新审视量子机器操作任务，提出回收测试轮次数据来持续监控噪声模型参数。这些测试轮次数据原本是为提供安全性而收集的，现在被重新利用来优化协议效率。

Result: 展示了量子通信验证协议的重复开销可以进一步减轻，通过回收测试数据实现噪声模型参数的持续监控。这体现了这些协议的多功能性，其模板可以服务于多个目的。

Conclusion: 该工作表明量子通信验证协议具有多功能性，其模板可以同时满足验证和监控需求，增加了早期集成到量子机器开发路线图中的吸引力，为服务提供商提供了更高效的验证方案。

Abstract: Interactive verification protocols for quantum computations allow to build trust between a client and a service provider, ensuring the former that the instructed computation was carried out faithfully. They come in two variants, one without quantum communication that requires large overhead on the server side to coherently implement quantum-resistant cryptographic primitives, and one with quantum communication but with repetition as the only overhead on the service provider's side. Given the limited number of available qubits on current machines, only quantum communication-based protocols have yielded proof of concepts.
  In this work, we show that the repetition overhead of protocols with quantum communication can be further mitigated if one examines the task of operating a quantum machine from the service provider's point of view. Indeed, we show that the test rounds data, whose collection is necessary to provide security, can indeed be recycled to perform continuous monitoring of noise model parameters for the service provider. This exemplifies the versatility of these protocols, whose template can serve multiple purposes and increases the interest in considering their early integration into development roadmaps of quantum machines.

</details>


### [132] [LO-Free Phase and Amplitude Recovery of an RF Signal with a DC-Stark-Enabled Rydberg Receiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30023)
*Vladislav Katkov,Nikola Zlatanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需射频本振的里德堡原子接收器理论框架，通过直流偏置激活相位敏感环路，实现单个射频信号的幅度和载波相位恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统里德堡原子接收器需要注入射频本振(LO)才能进行相干接收，这增加了系统复杂性。本文旨在开发一种无需辅助射频LO的相干接收方法，简化系统配置。

Method: 通过施加静态直流偏置，使近简并里德堡对发生斯塔克混合，激活原本缺失的上光学通路，形成仅由标准探针/耦合对和接收射频场驱动的相位敏感环路。推导了均匀偏置下的四能级旋转框架Floquet哈密顿量，分析了周期性稳态的精确谐波相位规律。

Result: 证明了第n个探针谐波携带因子e^{inΦ_S}，可直接从解调的探针谐波估计信号相位和幅度。在高信噪比下推导了RMSE定律，识别了相位最优和幅度最优的偏置控制混合角，并提出了加权联合设计准则和平衡折衷角。扩展分析了非均匀偏置的准静态空间平均效应。

Conclusion: 该框架为无需辅助射频LO的单个射频信号相干里德堡接收提供了最小化路径，通过直流偏置激活相位敏感机制，简化了系统配置并保持了相干接收能力。

Abstract: We present a theoretical framework for recovering the amplitude and carrier phase of a single received RF field with a Rydberg-atom receiver, without injecting an RF local oscillator (LO) into the atoms. The key enabling mechanism is a static DC bias applied to the vapor cell: by Stark-mixing a near-degenerate Rydberg pair, the bias activates an otherwise absent upper optical pathway and closes a phase-sensitive loop within a receiver driven only by the standard probe/coupling pair and the received RF field. For a spatially uniform bias, we derive an effective four-level rotating-frame Hamiltonian of Floquet form and show that the periodic steady state obeys an exact harmonic phase law, so that the $n$th probe harmonic carries the factor $e^{inΦ_S}$. This yields direct estimators for the signal phase and amplitude from a demodulated probe harmonic, with amplitude recovery obtained by inverting an injective harmonic response map. In the high-SNR regime, we derive explicit RMSE laws and use them to identify distinct phase-optimal and amplitude-optimal bias-controlled mixing angles, together with a weighted joint-design criterion and a balanced compromise angle that equalizes the fractional phase and amplitude penalties. We then extend the analysis to nonuniform DC bias through quasistatic spatial averaging and show that bias inhomogeneity reduces coherent gain for phase readout while also reshaping the amplitude-response slope. Numerical examples validate the phase law, illustrate response-map inversion and mixing-angle trade-offs, and quantify the penalties induced by bias nonuniformity. The results establish a minimal route to coherent Rydberg reception of a single RF signal without an auxiliary RF LO in the atoms.

</details>
